import {
  Navigate,
  Outlet,
  createRootRoute,
  createRoute,
  createRouter,
  useParams,
} from '@tanstack/react-router';

import { AdminVisitorPage } from './features/admin/AdminVisitorPage';
import { GuardApp } from './features/guard/GuardApp';
import { LobbyPage } from './features/lobby/LobbyPage';
import { VisitorPassPage } from './features/visitor/VisitorPassPage';
import { WalkInRegisterPage } from './features/visitor/WalkInRegisterPage';

const rootRoute = createRootRoute({
  component: Outlet,
  notFoundComponent: () => <Navigate to="/guard" />,
});

const indexRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/',
  component: () => <Navigate to="/guard" />,
});

const guardRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/guard',
  component: GuardApp,
});

const guardRegisterRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/guard/register',
  component: WalkInRegisterPage,
});

const guardPassRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/guard/checkin/$token',
  component: GuardPassPage,
});

const guardLobbyRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/guard/lobby',
  component: LobbyPage,
});

const registerRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/visitor/register',
  component: WalkInRegisterPage,
});

const passRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/visitor/checkin/$token',
  component: VisitorPassRoute,
});

const lobbyRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/visitor/lobby',
  component: LobbyPage,
});

const adminRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/dashboard',
  component: AdminVisitorPage,
});

const legacyAdminRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/admin/visitor',
  component: () => <Navigate to="/dashboard" replace />,
});

const routeTree = rootRoute.addChildren([
  indexRoute,
  guardRoute,
  guardRegisterRoute,
  guardPassRoute,
  guardLobbyRoute,
  registerRoute,
  passRoute,
  lobbyRoute,
  adminRoute,
  legacyAdminRoute,
]);

export const router = createRouter({
  routeTree,
  defaultPreload: 'intent',
  scrollRestoration: true,
});

declare module '@tanstack/react-router' {
  interface Register {
    router: typeof router;
  }
}

function GuardPassPage() {
  const { token } = useParams({ from: '/guard/checkin/$token' });
  return <VisitorPassPage token={token} />;
}

function VisitorPassRoute() {
  const { token } = useParams({ from: '/visitor/checkin/$token' });
  return <VisitorPassPage token={token} />;
}
```

> AGENT

We do — `visitor-web` (the guard PWA) already uses TanStack Router. See `visitor-web/src/router.tsx`, which defines multiple guard routes:

- `/guard` → `GuardApp`
- `/guard/register` → walk-in registration
- `/guard/checkin/$token` → visitor pass by token (deep-linkable from QR)
- `/guard/lobby` → lobby view

Plus visitor-facing routes (`/visitor/...`) and `/dashboard` for admin, all in the same SPA.

Routing is needed here because the QR-scan flow hands the guard a token-bearing URL (`/guard/checkin/$token`) that has to be deep-linkable and shareable — that only works with real routes, not view state. The `nginx-spa.conf.template` is the server-side half of that (rewrites unknown paths to `index.html`).

Were you expecting the table in `CLAUDE.md` to say otherwise, or did something in the app behave like routing wasn't wired up?

> AGENT

You're right — that's an actual gap, not by design. The router only defines guard *entry points* (`/guard`, `/guard/register`, `/guard/checkin/$token`, `/guard/lobby`). Once inside `GuardApp`, the tab is plain component state:

`visitor-web/src/features/guard/GuardApp.tsx:59`
```ts
type GuardScreen = 'login' | 'scanner' | 'visitors' | 'manual' | 'expected' | 'settings';
```
`visitor-web/src/features/guard/GuardApp.tsx:140`
```ts
const [screen, setScreen] = useState<GuardScreen>('login');
```

Tabs flip via `setScreen(...)`, so the URL never changes. Consequences:

- Browser back/forward doesn't move between tabs
- Refresh always drops you back at `login → scanner`, losing the tab you were on
- Can't bookmark or share a link to "visitors" / "settings"
- PostHog pageview tracking under-reports — it only fires on the entry route

For a PWA where guards may refresh, reopen from the home-screen icon, or get a link from someone, URL-routed tabs are the right default. Two ways to fix:

1. **Quickest** — add child routes (`/guard/scan`, `/guard/visitors`, `/guard/expected`, `/guard/settings`) under a `guardRoute` layout, replace `screen` state with `useRouterState` / `Link`. Keeps `GuardApp` as the shell.
2. **Minimal churn** — keep `useState` but mirror it to a search param (`/guard?tab=visitors`) via `useNavigate({ search })`. Less idiomatic but a one-screen change.

Want me to go with option 1?

252	    window.addEventListener('online', onOnline);
253	    return () => {
254	      window.removeEventListener('offline', onOffline);
255	      window.removeEventListener('online', onOnline);
256	    };
257	  }, [showToast, t]);
258	
259	  useEffect(() => {
260	    const handler = () => setUpdateReady(true);
261	    window.addEventListener('app-update-ready', handler);
262	    return () => window.removeEventListener('app-update-ready', handler);
263	  }, []);
264	
265	  useEffect(() => {
266	    const onMessage = (event: MessageEvent) => {
267	      if (event.data?.type !== 'offline-sync-complete') return;
268	      setLastSyncSummary(event.data.summary ?? null);
269	      void refreshPendingCount();
270	    };
271	    navigator.serviceWorker?.addEventListener?.('message', onMessage);
272	    return () => navigator.serviceWorker?.removeEventListener?.('message', onMessage);
273	  }, [refreshPendingCount]);
274	
275	  useEffect(() => {
276	    if (didRestoreSession.current) return;
277	    didRestoreSession.current = true;
278	
279	    async function restoreSession() {
280	      const savedAuth = (await loadGuardAuth()) as { token?: string; guardName?: string; guardId?: string } | null;
281	      const savedToken = getGuardToken() || savedAuth?.token || null;
282	      setBootMessage(savedToken ? t('restoring_session') : t('loading'));
283	      await refreshPendingCount();
284	
285	      if (!savedToken) {
286	        setScreen('login');
287	        setBooting(false);
288	        return;
289	      }
290	
291	      setGuardToken(savedToken);
292	      if (savedAuth?.guardName) localStorage.setItem('guard_name', savedAuth.guardName);
293	      if (savedAuth?.guardId) localStorage.setItem('guard_id', savedAuth.guardId);
294	
295	      if (!navigator.onLine) {
296	        setScreen('scanner');
297	        setBooting(false);
298	        return;
299	      }
300	
301	      setBootMessage('Checking saved sign-in...');
302	      const session = await getGuardSession();
303	      if (session.ok) {
304	        if (session.data.guardName) localStorage.setItem('guard_name', session.data.guardName);
305	        if (session.data.guardId) localStorage.setItem('guard_id', session.data.guardId);
306	        await saveGuardAuth({
307	          token: savedToken,
308	          guardId: session.data.guardId || null,
309	          guardName: session.data.guardName || savedAuth?.guardName || null,
310	        });
311	        if (session.data.language) setLang(session.data.language === 'hindi' ? 'hi' : 'en');
312	        setScreen('scanner');
313	        void registerBackgroundSync();
314	        void attemptQueueSync({ reason: 'startup', silentWhenEmpty: true, silentSummary: true });
315	      } else if (session.status === 401) {
316	        await clearSavedGuardSession();
317	        setScreen('login');
318	      } else {
319	        setScreen('scanner');
320	      }
321	      setBooting(false);
322	    }
323	
324	    void restoreSession();
325	  }, [refreshPendingCount]);
326	
327	  useEffect(() => {
328	    const onExpired = () => {
329	      void (async () => {
330	        await clearSavedGuardSession();
331	        setScreen('login');
332	        showToast(t('visitors_error_auth'), 'error', 'alert');
333	      })();
334	    };
335	    window.addEventListener('appointy:guard-session-expired', onExpired);
336	    return () => window.removeEventListener('appointy:guard-session-expired', onExpired);
337	  }, [showToast, t]);
338	
339	  async function clearSavedGuardSession() {
340	    clearGuardToken();
341	    localStorage.removeItem('guard_id');
342	    await clearGuardAuth();
343	  }
344	
345	  async function attemptQueueSync({
346	    reason = 'manual',
347	    announceReconnect = false,
348	    silentWhenEmpty = false,
349	    silentSummary = false,
350	  }: {
351	    reason?: string;
352	    announceReconnect?: boolean;
353	    silentWhenEmpty?: boolean;
354	    silentSummary?: boolean;
355	  } = {}) {
356	    const token = getGuardToken();
357	    if (!token) {
358	      await refreshPendingCount();
359	      return null;
360	    }
361	
362	    if (!navigator.onLine) {
363	      if (!silentSummary) showToast(t('sync_retry_unavailable'), 'error', 'wifi-off');
364	      await refreshPendingCount();
365	      return null;
366	    }
367	
368	    if (syncInProgress) return lastSyncSummary;
369	    const pendingBefore = await refreshPendingCount();
370	    if (pendingBefore === 0) {
371	      const summary = { reason, at: new Date().toISOString(), pendingBefore: 0, success: 0, failed: 0, review: 0 };
372	      setLastSyncSummary(summary);
373	      if (announceReconnect) showToast(t('back_online'), 'success', 'wifi');
374	      if (!silentWhenEmpty && !announceReconnect) showToast(t('sync_idle'), 'info', 'info');
375	      return summary;
376	    }
377	
378	    setSyncInProgress(true);
379	    showToast(interpolate(t('sync_replay_started'), { count: pendingBefore }), 'info', 'refresh');
380	    try {
381	      const result = await processQueue(token);
382	      const summary = {
383	        reason,
384	        at: new Date().toISOString(),
385	        pendingBefore,
386	        success: result.success,
387	        failed: result.failed,
388	        review: result.review,
389	        results: result.results,
390	      };
391	      setLastSyncSummary(summary);
392	      await refreshPendingCount();
393	      if (!silentSummary || result.success > 0 || result.failed > 0 || result.review > 0) {
394	        showToast(
395	          result.failed === 0 && result.review === 0
396	            ? t('sync_success_portal')
397	            : interpolate(t('sync_replay_summary'), {
398	              success: result.success,
399	              review: result.review,
400	              failed: result.failed,
401	            }),
402	          result.failed > 0 ? 'error' : result.review > 0 ? 'info' : 'success',
403	          result.failed > 0 ? 'alert' : result.review > 0 ? 'info' : 'check',
404	        );
405	      }
406	      return summary;
407	    } finally {
408	      setSyncInProgress(false);
409	    }
410	  }
411	
412	  return (
413	    <>
414	      <div id="app-boot" aria-live="polite">
415	        <div className="app-boot-card">
416	          <div className="spinner" aria-hidden="true" />
417	          <div>{bootMessage}</div>
418	        </div>
419	      </div>
420	      <div id="offline-banner">{t('offline_banner')}</div>
421	      {updateReady && (
422	        <div id="update-banner" role="button" onClick={() => window.location.reload()}>
423	          <RefreshCw size={14} strokeWidth={2.5} aria-hidden />
424	          <span>{t('update_banner')}</span>
425	        </div>
426	      )}
427	      <div id="toast" className={toast ? `show ${toast.tone}` : ''}>
428	        {toast?.icon ? <span className="toast-icon"><IconGlyph name={toast.icon} /></span> : null}
429	        {toast?.message ? <span>{toast.message}</span> : null}
430	      </div>
431	      <div id="app">
432	        {screen === 'login' ? (
433	          <LoginScreen
434	            lang={lang}
435	            setLang={setLang}
436	            t={t}
437	            showToast={showToast}
438	            onLoggedIn={async (token, guardName, guardId) => {
439	              setGuardToken(token);
440	              localStorage.setItem('guard_name', guardName);
441	              if (guardId) localStorage.setItem('guard_id', guardId);
442	              await saveGuardAuth({ token, guardId: guardId || null, guardName });
443	              setScreen('scanner');
444	              void registerBackgroundSync();
445	              void attemptQueueSync({ reason: 'login', silentWhenEmpty: true, silentSummary: true });
446	            }}
447	          />
448	        ) : (
449	          <>
450	            {screen === 'scanner' ? (
451	              <ScannerScreen
452	                t={t}
453	                showToast={showToast}
454	                pendingCount={pendingCount}
455	                syncInProgress={syncInProgress}
456	                online={online}
457	                attemptQueueSync={attemptQueueSync}
458	                refreshPendingCount={refreshPendingCount}
459	              />
460	            ) : null}
461	            {screen === 'visitors' ? <VisitorsScreen t={t} showToast={showToast} online={online} refreshPendingCount={refreshPendingCount} /> : null}
462	            {screen === 'manual' ? <ManualScreen t={t} showToast={showToast} online={online} refreshPendingCount={refreshPendingCount} /> : null}
463	            {screen === 'expected' ? <ExpectedGuestsScreen t={t} showToast={showToast} online={online} /> : null}
464	            {screen === 'settings' ? (
465	              <SettingsScreen
466	                t={t}
467	                lang={lang}
468	                setLang={setLang}
469	                pendingCount={pendingCount}
470	                lastSyncSummary={lastSyncSummary}
471	                syncInProgress={syncInProgress}
472	                attemptQueueSync={attemptQueueSync}
473	                onLogout={async () => {
474	                  await guardLogout();
475	                  await clearSavedGuardSession();
476	                  setScreen('login');
477	                  await refreshPendingCount();
478	                }}
479	              />
480	            ) : null}
481	            {canInstallApp ? <InstallAppButton t={t} onInstall={handleInstallApp} /> : null}
482	            <BottomNav active={screen} setScreen={setScreen} t={t} pendingCount={pendingCount} />
483	          </>
484	        )}
485	        {screen === 'login' && canInstallApp ? <InstallAppButton t={t} onInstall={handleInstallApp} /> : null}
486	      </div>
487	    </>
488	  );
489	}
490	
491	function LoginScreen({
492	  lang,
493	  setLang,
494	  t,
495	  showToast,
496	  onLoggedIn,
497	}: {
498	  lang: Lang;
499	  setLang: (lang: Lang) => void;

559	function ScannerScreen({
560	  t,
561	  showToast,
562	  pendingCount,
563	  syncInProgress,
564	  online,
565	  attemptQueueSync,
566	  refreshPendingCount,
567	}: {
568	  t: (key: string) => string;
569	  showToast: (message: string, tone?: ToastTone, icon?: IconName) => void;
570	  pendingCount: number;
571	  syncInProgress: boolean;
572	  online: boolean;
573	  attemptQueueSync: (options?: SyncOptions) => Promise<SyncSummary | null>;
574	  refreshPendingCount: () => Promise<number>;
575	}) {
576	  const [scannerActive, setScannerActive] = useState(true);
577	  const [restartKey, setRestartKey] = useState(0);
578	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
579	  const [currentCard, setCurrentCard] = useState<CurrentVisitorCard | null>(null);
580	
581	  const startScanner = useCallback(() => {
582	    setCurrentCard(null);
583	    setLoading(false);
584	    setScannerActive(true);
585	    setRestartKey((value) => value + 1);
586	  }, []);
587	
588	  async function handleScannedValue(value: string) {
589	    setScannerActive(false);
590	    const extracted = extractToken(value);
591	    if (!extracted.token) return;
592	    await handleQRResult(extracted);
593	  }
594	
595	  async function handleQRResult(extracted: ExtractedQrToken) {
596	    setLoading(true);
597	    const res = await verifyQr(extracted.token);
598	    setLoading(false);
599	
600	    if (res.offline) {
601	      const expectedMatch = findCachedExpectedGuestByToken(extracted.token);
602	      const visitorMeta = expectedMatch
603	        ? expectedGuestToMeta(expectedMatch)
604	        : extracted.visitorMeta;
605	      if (expectedMatch) showToast(t('expected_match_found'), 'info', 'search');
606	      setCurrentCard({ token: extracted.token, offlinePending: true, visitorMeta });
607	      return;
608	    }
609	    if (!res.data?.invitation) {
610	      setCurrentCard({ token: extracted.token, data: res.data });
611	      return;
612	    }
613	    const card = { token: extracted.token, data: res.data };
614	    setCurrentCard(card);
615	    if (
616	      res.ok &&
617	      res.data.valid &&
618	      res.data.invitation.approval_status === 'approved' &&
619	      !res.data.session
620	    ) {
621	      showToast(t('auto_checking_in'), 'info', 'check');
622	      await checkIn(card);
623	    }
624	  }
625	
626	  async function checkIn(card: CurrentVisitorCard) {
627	    const res = await checkInVisitor(card.token);
628	    if (res.ok) {
629	      showToast(t('checked_in'), 'success', 'check');
630	      setCurrentCard({ ...card, data: { ...card.data!, session: res.data.session } });
631	      return;
632	    }
633	    if (res.offline) {
634	      const invitation = card.data?.invitation;
635	      const event = createOfflineEvent({
636	        eventType: 'qr_checkin_capture',
637	        ...getGuardContext(),
638	        qrToken: card.token,
639	        scannedRawValue: card.token,
640	        visitorName: invitation?.visitor_name || undefined,
641	        hostName: invitation?.host_employee_name || undefined,
642	        purpose: invitation?.purpose || undefined,
643	      }) as OfflineEventRecord;
644	      const recordId = await enqueue(event) as number;
645	      await refreshPendingCount();
646	      void registerBackgroundSync();
647	      setCurrentCard({ ...card, offlinePending: true, offlineEventId: event.offlineEventId });
648	      showToast(t('checkin_saved_offline'), 'info', 'offline');
649	      return;
650	    }
651	    showToast(res.data?.errorMessage ?? t('error'), 'error', 'alert');
652	  }
653	
654	  async function saveOfflineQr(card: CurrentVisitorCard, form: OfflineQrFormValue) {
655	    const event = createOfflineEvent({
656	      eventType: 'qr_checkin_capture',
657	      ...getGuardContext(),
658	      qrToken: card.token,
659	      scannedRawValue: card.token,
660	      visitorName: form.visitorName || undefined,
661	      hostName: form.hostName || undefined,
662	      purpose: form.purpose || undefined,
663	      notes: form.notes || undefined,
664	    }) as OfflineEventRecord;
665	    const recordId = await enqueue(event) as number;
666	    await refreshPendingCount();
667	    void registerBackgroundSync();
668	    setCurrentCard({ ...card, offlinePending: true, offlineEventId: event.offlineEventId });
669	    showToast(t('checkin_saved_offline'), 'info', 'offline');
670	    return recordId;
671	  }
672	
673	  return (
674	    <div id="scanner-screen" className="screen active" aria-label={t('aria_scanner')}>
675	      <Topbar title={t('page_scan')} icon="camera">
676	        <button className="icon-btn" type="button" title={t('aria_restart_camera')} aria-label={t('aria_restart_camera')} onClick={startScanner}>
677	          <IconGlyph name="refresh" />
678	        </button>
679	      </Topbar>
680	      <div className="scanner-body">
681	        <QrScanner active={scannerActive} restartKey={restartKey} onResult={handleScannedValue} />
682	        <div className="scan-hint">{t('scan_hint')}</div>
683	        {(syncInProgress || pendingCount > 0) ? (
684	          <ScannerSyncPanel
685	            t={t}
686	            pendingCount={pendingCount}
687	            syncInProgress={syncInProgress}
688	            online={online}
689	            attemptQueueSync={attemptQueueSync}
690	          />
691	        ) : null}
692	        {loading ? <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', padding: 24 }}><div className="spinner" style={{ margin: '0 auto' }} /></div> : null}
693	        {currentCard ? (
694	          <div id="visitor-card-section">
695	            <VisitorResultCard
696	              card={currentCard}
697	              t={t}
698	              onRetry={() => handleQRResult({ token: currentCard.token, visitorMeta: currentCard.visitorMeta ?? null })}
699	              onRescan={startScanner}

'active' : ''}`}>\n          <span className=\"nav-icon\" style={tab.id === 'scanner' ? { position: 'relative' } : undefined}>\n            <IconGlyph name={tab.icon} />\n            {tab.id === 'scanner' ? <span className=\"queue-badge\" style={{ display: pendingCount > 0 ? 'flex' : 'none' }}>{pendingCount > 99 ? '99+' : pendingCount || ''}</span> : null}\n          </span>\n          <span>{t(tab.labelKey)}</span>\n        </Link>\n      ))}\n    </nav>\n  );\n}"
}

Build passes. Summary of what changed:

**`visitor-web/src/router.tsx`**
- Added pathless layout route `guardShellRoute` whose component is `GuardApp`. Child tab routes under it: `/guard` (index), `/guard/scan`, `/guard/visitors`, `/guard/manual`, `/guard/expected`, `/guard/settings`. These render `null` — the shell handles the body.
- Kept `/guard/register`, `/guard/checkin/$token`, `/guard/lobby` as siblings of the shell (they are not inside the tab UI).

**`visitor-web/src/features/guard/GuardApp.tsx`**
- Replaced `screen` state with `authed: boolean`. Tab is now derived from `useLocation().pathname` via `tabFromPathname()`.
- `BottomNav` now uses TanStack `<Link>` instead of `<button onClick={setScreen(...)}>`, so each tab click changes the URL (and updates browser history).
- Post-login: stays at the URL the user requested (e.g. refreshing on `/guard/settings` lands you back on settings). Only navigates to `/guard/scan` if you logged in from `/` or `/guard`.
- Logout navigates to `/guard`.

Since the layout route keeps `GuardApp` mounted across tab navigations, the camera, login state, sync state etc. don't reset on tab switch.

`bun run build` is clean — I haven't manually clicked through the tabs in the browser. `visitor-web/CLAUDE.md` is explicit that `tsc` passing ≠ feature works, so worth a quick `bun run dev` and clicking around (scan ↔ visitors ↔ manual ↔ expected ↔ settings) to confirm URLs update and browser back/forward behave.

Perfect! Let me create a summary report now:

## Summary: Manual Visitor Entry → Host Notification Feature Mapping

### Frontend (visitor-web)

**1. ManualScreen Component**  
Location: `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/visitor-web/src/features/guard/GuardApp.tsx:976–1097`

- **Fields captured:**
  - `visitor_name` (required, free-text)
  - `phone` (optional, free-text)
  - `purposeType` (picklist: `meeting`, `interview`, `delivery`, `other`)
  - `customPurpose` (required when `purposeType=other`, free-text)
  - `host` (required when `purposeType=meeting|interview`, free-text autocomplete via `HostSuggestInput`)

- **Host-implying purposes:** `meeting`, `interview` (line 987: `requiresHost = form.purposeType === 'meeting' || form.purposeType === 'interview'`)
- **Purpose picklist:** Lines 967–972, maps frontend labels to `backendLabel` (e.g., `meeting` → `"Meeting"`)

**2. API Client Function**  
Location: `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/visitor-web/src/api/visitor.ts:212–214`

```typescript
export function manualEntry(input: { visitor_name: string; phone?: string; purpose?: string; host?: string }) {
  return apiRequest<{ success: boolean; message?: string; error?: string }>
    ('POST', '/api/visitor/manual-entry', input, { guard: true });
}
```

- **Endpoint:** `POST /api/visitor/manual-entry`
- **Payload shape:** flat object with `visitor_name`, `phone?`, `purpose?`, `host?`

---

### Backend (office-ops-engine)

**3. HTTP Route Handler**  
Location: `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/visitor/routes.ts:3306–3356`

**What it does today:**
- Requires guard auth (line 3307)
- Normalizes `visitor_name` (required)
- **Attempts host resolution** (lines 3318–3326):
  - By `hostEmployeeId` → looks up in staff
  - By `hostPhone`/`hostEmail` → queries contact lookup
  - Falls back to free-text `host` name if neither matches
- **Creates visitor invite** via `createVisitorInvite()` with `approvalTier: 'guard_entry'` and `approvalStatus: 'approved'` (lines 3328–3339)
- **Immediately checks in visitor** via `checkInVisitor(invite.qrToken)` (lines 3342–3343)
- Returns 201 with `invitationId` and session

**Key insight:** Manual entry skips normal invitation flow — it auto-approves and auto-checks-in, so host notification is **not currently triggered**.

**4. Visitor Purpose/Reason Enum**  
Backend does not enforce an enum; it accepts the string value from frontend (e.g., `"Meeting"`, `"Interview"`, `"Delivery"`, custom string for `"Other"`).

**Suggestion for identifying host-requiring reasons:**
- Frontend already marks `meeting` and `interview` as requiring a host (line 987).
- Backend could check `purpose IN ('Meeting', 'Interview')` to identify employee-host visits.

**5. Employee/Staff Directory**  
Location: `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/core/staff-registry.ts`

- **Lookup functions:**
  - `getStaffByName(name: string)` (line 161) — exact or fuzzy match
  - `getStaffByContact({ phone?, email? })` (line 190–202) — by phone/email
  - `searchCachedStaff(query, limit)` (line 204–211) — scored fuzzy search
- **Staff record shape** (`StaffRecord`, `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/lib/types.ts:184–203`):
  - `id`, `name`, `email`, `phone`, `department`, `isActive`
  - **`channels?: StaffChannelLink[]`** — linked Slack/Telegram/WhatsApp identities

---

### **6. Slack Integration**

**Slack Client:** `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/channels/slack/client.ts`

**Available functions:**
- **Send DM to user:** `sendSlackDm({ userId, text, blocks? })` (line 114–131)
  - Opens 1-to-1 conversation, sends message
  - Returns `SlackMessageRef | null` if configured
- **Lookup user by email:** `getSlackUserByEmail(email)` (line 210–245)
  - Returns `SlackUserProfile` with `userId`, `username`, display names
  - Returns `null` if not found or Slack disabled

**Config:** `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/lib/config`
- Reads `SLACK_BOT_TOKEN` env var; gracefully no-ops if missing

**Existing pattern:** `host-notifier.ts` sends Slack DMs with interactive buttons when scheduled visitors arrive (lines 188–212). Uses `sendSlackDm()` to resolve staff Slack ID and send formatted message.

---

### **7. Email Integration**

**Email Provider:** `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/visitor/email-provider.ts:1–100`

- **Unified adapter:** picks Resend (HTTP) or SMTP (Nodemailer) via `EMAIL_PROVIDER` env var
- **Default:** SMTP (backwards compatible)
- **Function:** `sendMail({ to, subject, text?, html?, attachments? })` returns `{ sent: boolean, error?, id? }`
- **From address:** falls back from `EMAIL_FROM` → `SMTP_FROM` → `SMTP_USER` → `noreply@appointy.com`
- **Graceful no-op:** if neither provider configured, returns `{ sent: false }`

**Existing usage:**
- `host-notifier.ts` sends email to host staff via `sendMail()` (line 297)
- `email-sender.ts` sends visitor pass PDFs
- All email infrastructure is already in place and tested

---

### **8. Architecture Guidance**

Location: `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/ARCHITECTURE.md`

**Key principles for this feature:**
- New host-notification logic should live in the **visitor module** (plugin pattern), not core
- Host notification is a **side effect** of manual entry → trigger it directly in the route handler or via the flow-pack system
- No new domain/channel needed; reuse existing `host-notifier.ts` patterns

---

### **9. Existing Host Notification Pattern (Reusable!)**

Location: `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/visitor/host-notifier.ts:331–381`

**Public function:** `dmPrimaryHostsOnArrival(invitation, sessionId, locationName?, options?)`
- Splits comma-separated host names
- Resolves each to staff member (fuzzy name match)
- **Sends Slack DM** (with response buttons) if staff has Slack channel linked
- **Sends Telegram DM** (with buttons) if staff has Telegram channel linked
- Returns `true` if at least one host was notified

**This is already used in the flow-pack system** for QR-based check-ins (lines 215–236 of `flow-pack-actions.ts`).

**Reusable notification helper:** `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/visitor/notification-dispatcher.ts`
- `notifyAdminsOnHostVisitorArrival()` — sends email + Slack/Telegram DMs to admins
- Email uses template from `getVisitorUiConfig()`

---

## **Surprises & Blockers**

✓ **Good news:** All infra exists.
- Slack DM sending, email sending, staff lookup, host-resolution helpers all in place
- Manual-entry route already creates a visitor invite and checks them in
- Pattern for host notification already proven (QR-based flow)

✓ **Email addresses are available** on `StaffRecord.email` (filled from staff directory sync)

⚠ **Design decision:** When a guard files a **manual** entry with a host-implying purpose (interview, meeting), the route currently does NOT trigger host notification. It just creates & approves the invite, then checks the visitor in.

**To implement the feature:**
1. In `routes.ts:3350` (after successful check-in), call `dmPrimaryHostsOnArrival(invite, session.sessionId, ...)`
2. Or, queue a flow rule to run `dmPrimaryHostsOnArrival` + email dispatch
3. Identify host-requiring reasons: check `purpose IN ('Meeting', 'Interview')`
4. Use `host-notifier.ts` + `email-sender.ts` for notifications (both already handle Slack + email)
```

246	export async function notifyHostStakeholders(
247	  invitation: VisitorInvitation,
248	  sessionId: string,
249	  now: string,
250	  locationName?: string,
251	  includePrimaryHost = false,
252	): Promise<boolean> {
253	  const db = getOptionalPostgresDb();
254	  if (!db) return false;
255	
256	  const stakeholders = await db
257	    .select()
258	    .from(visitorInvitationStakeholders)
259	    .where(eq(visitorInvitationStakeholders.invitationId, invitation.id));
260	
261	  const notifyHosts = stakeholders.filter((stakeholder) => {
262	    if (stakeholder.role === 'primary_host' && !includePrimaryHost) return false;
263	    if (stakeholder.role !== 'cc_host' && stakeholder.role !== 'primary_host') return false;
264	    const notifyOn = Array.isArray(stakeholder.notifyOn) ? stakeholder.notifyOn : [];
265	    return notifyOn.includes('arrival');
266	  });
267	  if (notifyHosts.length === 0) return false;
268	
269	  const lines = [
270	    `${invitation.visitor_name} has arrived.`,
271	    invitation.purpose ? `Purpose: ${invitation.purpose}` : null,
272	    invitation.host_employee_name ? `Primary host: ${invitation.host_employee_name}` : null,
273	    locationName ? `Location: ${locationName}` : null,
274	    `Arrived at: ${now}`,
275	  ].filter(Boolean) as string[];
276	  const text = lines.join('\n');
277	  let notified = false;
278	
279	  for (const stakeholder of notifyHosts) {
280	    if (stakeholder.slackUserId) {
281	      try {
282	        const slackRef = await sendSlackDm({
283	          userId: stakeholder.slackUserId,
284	          text: `Visitor arrived: ${invitation.visitor_name}`,
285	          blocks: [{
286	            type: 'section',
287	            text: { type: 'mrkdwn', text },
288	          }],
289	        });
290	        notified = notified || !!slackRef?.ts;
291	      } catch (error) {
292	        console.error(`[HostNotifier] Failed to DM Slack CC host ${stakeholder.email ?? stakeholder.name}:`, error);
293	      }
294	    }
295	
296	    if (stakeholder.email && isEmailConfigured()) {
297	      const result = await sendMail({
298	        to: stakeholder.email,
299	        subject: `Visitor arrived: ${invitation.visitor_name}`,
300	        text,
301	      });
302	      if (result.sent) {
303	        notified = true;
304	      } else if (result.error) {
305	        console.error(`[HostNotifier] Failed to email CC host ${stakeholder.email}:`, result.error);
306	      }
307	    }
308	  }
309	
310	  if (notified) {
311	    await logAction({
312	      action: 'notification_sent',
313	      actorType: 'system',
314	      channel: 'stakeholder',
315	      sessionId,
316	      invitationId: invitation.id,
317	      metadata: { event: 'host_stakeholder_arrival', recipients: notifyHosts.map((item) => item.email ?? item.name).filter(Boolean) },
318	    });
319	  }
320	
321	  return notified;
322	}
323	
324	// ── Extracted primitive: DM each primary host on arrival ─────────────
325	// Loops over the invitation's comma-separated host names, resolves each
326	// to a staff record (or falls back to email→Slack), and DMs them on
327	// Telegram + Slack with response buttons. Returns whether at least one
328	// host was notified successfully. Pure side-effect — no DB writes,
329	// no audit log, no admin escalation. Composable for the
330	// `visitor.on_checkin` default automation.

Before I write code, I need to align on a few choices — this touches both behavior and surface area.

**What's in place (good news):**
- Manual-entry route lives at `office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/visitor/routes.ts:3306`. It already resolves the host to a `StaffRecord` and creates an invite. It just doesn't notify anyone.
- `dmPrimaryHostsOnArrival(invitation, sessionId, ...)` in `host-notifier.ts:331` already does Slack DM (also Telegram, with response buttons). Resolves comma-separated host names to staff via fuzzy match.
- `sendMail({ to, subject, text })` in `email-provider.ts` is wired and gracefully no-ops if email isn't configured. `StaffRecord.email` is populated.

**What's NOT in place:**
- `dmPrimaryHostsOnArrival` does not send email today (only Slack/Telegram). Email is only sent to *stakeholders* (CC hosts on scheduled invites) — manual entries don't have those rows.

**Decisions I need from you:**

1. **Trigger condition.** Frontend defines `requiresHost = purposeType ∈ {meeting, interview}` (`ManualScreen` at `GuardApp.tsx:987`). I'll mirror that on the backend: notify if `purpose ∈ {Meeting, Interview}` AND we resolved a host staff member. Sound right? (Or: notify whenever a host was resolved, regardless of purpose?)

2. **Telegram.** You said "Slack + email". The existing helper DMs both Slack *and* Telegram. Three options:
   - (a) Slack + email only — skip Telegram (literal reading)
   - (b) Slack + Telegram + email — reuse the existing DM helper, just add email
   - (c) Whatever channels the staff has linked — same as (b) in practice
   
   I'd lean (b)/(c): if a staff member has Telegram linked, they probably prefer it over email. But your call.

3. **Where the email logic lives.** Add it to `dmPrimaryHostsOnArrival` (then QR check-ins also start emailing hosts — broader behavior change), or add a narrower helper `notifyHostOnManualEntry()` that wraps the DM + email (manual-entry only)? I'd lean narrower — easier to roll back, no surprise change to the QR flow.

4. **Message tone.** The existing Slack/Telegram DM includes response buttons ("Coming", "5 min", "Not expecting"). For a manual entry the visitor is already inside — those buttons are kind of pointless. Plain FYI message instead?

My recommendation, unless you say otherwise: trigger on `purpose ∈ {Meeting, Interview}` + resolved host; reuse the existing Slack/Telegram DM helper (buttons and all — simpler than diverging the templates); add a thin email send alongside; keep the change scoped to the manual-entry route so QR behavior is untouched.

OK to proceed on that, or adjust?

Perfect! Now I have all the information I need. Let me create a comprehensive report.

## Summary Report: Notification Recipients Setting Location & Usage

### 1. Frontend (ops-dashboard)

**Setting UI Location:** `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/ops-dashboard/src/main.tsx`, **lines 2444–2477**

**Field Structure:**
- **notifyStakeholderRoles** (string[]): Checkboxes for baseline roles:
  - `"host"` — Notify the employee being visited
  - `"approver"` — Notify admins/approval authorities
- **notifyEmails** (string[]): Freeform email list. Users add/remove emails (validated with `includes("@")`)

**API Endpoint:**
- **POST** `/api/admin/visitor/rules` (lines 2743–2788 in backend routes)
  - Request body via `saveRule()` (ops-dashboard/src/api/ops.ts:728–738)
  - Payload shape:
    ```
    {
      key: "visitor.walkin.approval",
      actions: {
        notifyStakeholderRoles: string[],
        notifyEmails: string[],
        ... other fields
      }
    }
    ```
  - Response: `{ success: true, rule: VisitorRule }`

### 2. Backend (office-ops-engine)

**Route Handler:** `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/visitor/routes.ts`
- **GET** `/api/admin/visitor/rules` (lines 2661–2669) — reads all rules
- **POST** `/api/admin/visitor/rules` (lines 2743–2788) — persists the rule to `visitorRules` table
- Calls `invalidateVisitorUiConfig()` to clear cache (line 2771)

**Storage:** PostgreSQL `visitorRules` table
- Row with `key = "visitor.walkin.approval"`
- `actions` JSONB field stores `{ notifyStakeholderRoles: [...], notifyEmails: [...], ... }`

**Reading the Setting:**
Use `getVisitorUiConfig()` in `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/visitor/policies.ts` (lines 651–730):
- Calls `getRuleAction("visitor.walkin.approval")` to fetch the rule actions
- Extracts via `asStringArray()` helpers (lines 702–709):
  ```typescript
  notifyStakeholderRoles: asStringArray(
    approvalRule?.notifyStakeholderRoles,
    DEFAULT_VISITOR_CONFIG.rules.notifyStakeholderRoles,
  ),
  notifyEmails: asStringArray(
    approvalRule?.notifyEmails,
    DEFAULT_VISITOR_CONFIG.rules.notifyEmails,
  ),
  ```
- Returns `VisitorUiConfig` with these arrays in `.rules` field (lines 249–258)

### 3. Notification Usage

**Email Notifications to Recipients:**
`sendNotifyEmails()` function in `/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/visitor/notification-dispatcher.ts` (lines 367–396):
- Called when walk-in arrives (line 361)
- Sends **email only** (no Slack lookup currently)
- Recipients: pulled from `visitorConfig.rules.notifyEmails`
- Email subject: `"Walk-in arrival: {visitorName}"` or `"[Escalation] Walk-in pending: ..."`
- **Does NOT use** `notifyStakeholderRoles` at send time (that's for approval routing, not walk-in emails)

**Admin Notifications (Separate):**
`notifyAdminsOnHostVisitorArrival()` (lines 464–514) sends **Telegram + Slack DMs** to admins (hardcoded `tgConfig.adminIds()` + `slackConfig.adminUserIds()`) — **not** via the baseline recipients setting.

**Slack User Lookup:**
`getSlackUserByEmail()` exists (`/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/channels/slack/client.ts`, line 210) but is **NOT currently used** in walk-in email flow. Email-to-Slack lookup would need to be added if you want to Slack DM the baseline recipients.

### Key Takeaway

The setting lives cleanly in **`visitor.walkin.approval` rule actions** (stored in Postgres, read via `getVisitorUiConfig()`). For your manual-entry route, call `getVisitorUiConfig()` to fetch `rules.notifyEmails` and `rules.notifyStakeholderRoles`, then dispatch emails via `sendNotifyEmails()` or build your own using the same email list. Slack DMing to recipients by email would require new email→Slack lookup logic.

Found the setting. It's `visitor.walkin.approval` rule (Postgres `visitorRules` table), read via `getVisitorUiConfig()` in `office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/visitor/policies.ts:651`. It has two fields:

- `notifyStakeholderRoles: string[]` — checkboxes for `"host"` and `"approver"`
- `notifyEmails: string[]` — additional baseline emails

Here's what I'll build. Confirm or correct before I start writing.

**Scope:** Just the guard manual-entry route at `routes.ts:3306`. (QR check-ins already DM the host via `dmPrimaryHostsOnArrival`; touching them would broaden behavior. Tell me if you want this on QR check-ins too.)

**Behavior** — after the manual entry succeeds, if a host was resolved on the invite:

1. Load `getVisitorUiConfig()` and read `rules.notifyStakeholderRoles` and `rules.notifyEmails`.
2. **If `notifyStakeholderRoles` includes `"host"`** → notify the resolved host:
   - Slack DM via their linked Slack channel on the staff record (or `getSlackUserByEmail(host.email)` fallback). Plain FYI message, no response buttons (visitor is already inside).
   - Email to `host.email`.
3. **Always email each address in `notifyEmails`** (these are baseline recipients — admins/facilities — regardless of host-checkbox state, since the user set them up specifically to receive walk-in alerts).
4. **`"approver"` role** is out of scope here (it's used by approval routing today, not this flow).
5. **Telegram** — skipped per your instruction.

**Notes / open choices:**
- For the `notifyEmails` baseline addresses, I'll send email only (no Slack lookup). Adding email→Slack lookup for them is easy if you want, but it changes those addresses' behavior — flag if yes.
- All sends are best-effort: failures get logged but don't fail the check-in response.
- New helper `notifyHostOnGuardManualEntry(invitation, sessionId, locationName?)` will live in `host-notifier.ts`. The route calls it after `checkInVisitor` succeeds.

if I want the employee suggestion in the guard PWA, to work even if the guard types names in hindi, or if types something that sound like the actual name but is not actual name, like Juneyd will show Junaid as a result, how can we do that, I actually want fast result also at low or zero cost

> AGENT

Cheapest path that meets your constraints: do it client-side with two layers — **transliterate Devanagari → Latin** (a small fixed table, or `sanscript` ~50KB gzipped) and then **score with Double Metaphone phonetic key + Levenshtein distance** against the staff list. The staff list is already small enough to fit in memory, so matching hundreds of names takes microseconds, works offline, and costs zero per query. "Juneyd" → "Junaid" hits via Double Metaphone (same key `JNT`); "जुनैद" → transliterate → `Junaid` → same key.

Tradeoff: Double Metaphone is English-tuned, so it nails Latin sound-alikes but can mis-rank certain Indian-origin names with unusual phonemes (rare consonant clusters, retroflex sounds). If you want richer Indic-aware matching later, Postgres `pg_trgm` + a small server endpoint is the next step up — one network hop, still nearly free — but I'd only reach for it if the client-side approach misses too often in practice.

Want me to wire the client-side version into `HostSuggestInput`, or hold off?

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/visitor-web/src/features/guard/nameMatch.ts",
  "content": "// Client-side fuzzy name matcher for the guard PWA.\n// Goals: cheap (no network), fast (microseconds), works offline,\n// tolerates Devanagari input + Latin sound-alikes / typos.\n//\n// Pipeline per candidate name:\n//   1. Lowercase + strip diacritics + transliterate any Devanagari\n//      to Latin (so \"जुनैद\" becomes \"junaid\").\n//   2. Compute a Soundex-style phonetic key (so \"Juneyd\" and \"Junaid\"\n//      collapse to the same key).\n//   3. Score: phonetic key match (cheap & strong), substring match,\n//      Levenshtein distance on the transliterated form.\n//\n// Bundle cost: ~2KB minified, zero deps.\n\n// Devanagari → Latin transliteration table. Covers the consonants,\n// vowels, matras and modifiers commonly seen in Indian names. Anything\n// outside this set is passed through unchanged so Latin input is a no-op.\nconst DEVANAGARI_MAP: Record<string, string> = {\n  // independent vowels\n  'अ': 'a', 'आ': 'aa', 'इ': 'i', 'ई': 'ii', 'उ': 'u', 'ऊ': 'uu',\n  'ऋ': 'ri', 'ए': 'e', 'ऐ': 'ai', 'ओ': 'o', 'औ': 'au',\n  // consonants\n  'क': 'k', 'ख': 'kh', 'ग': 'g', 'घ': 'gh', 'ङ': 'ng',\n  'च': 'ch', 'छ': 'chh', 'ज': 'j', 'झ': 'jh', 'ञ': 'ny',\n  'ट': 't', 'ठ': 'th', 'ड': 'd', 'ढ': 'dh', 'ण': 'n',\n  'त': 't', 'थ': 'th', 'द': 'd', 'ध': 'dh', 'न': 'n',\n  'प': 'p', 'फ': 'ph', 'ब': 'b', 'भ': 'bh', 'म': 'm',\n  'य': 'y', 'र': 'r', 'ल': 'l', 'व': 'v',\n  'श': 'sh', 'ष': 'sh', 'स': 's', 'ह': 'h',\n  // matras (dependent vowel signs)\n  'ा': 'a', 'ि': 'i', 'ी': 'ii', 'ु': 'u', 'ू': 'uu',\n  'ृ': 'ri', 'े': 'e', 'ै': 'ai', 'ो': 'o', 'ौ': 'au',\n  // modifiers\n  'ं': 'n', 'ः': 'h', 'ँ': 'n',\n  '्': '', // virama suppresses the implicit 'a'\n  '़': '', // nukta — ignore for matching\n};\n\nfunction transliterate(input: string): string {\n  let out = '';\n  for (const ch of input) {\n    out += DEVANAGARI_MAP[ch] ?? ch;\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nfunction normalize(input: string): string {\n  // strip combining diacritics (NFD then drop \\p{Mn})\n  return transliterate(input)\n    .normalize('NFD')\n    .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '')\n    .toLowerCase()\n    .replace(/[^a-z]+/g, '');\n}\n\n// Soundex-style key: first letter + 3 consonant-class digits.\n// Maps phonetically similar consonants to the same digit, so e.g.\n// \"Junaid\" and \"Juneyd\" both reduce to \"J530\".\nconst SOUNDEX_CODE: Record<string, string> = {\n  b: '1', f: '1', p: '1', v: '1',\n  c: '2', g: '2', j: '2', k: '2', q: '2', s: '2', x: '2', z: '2',\n  d: '3', t: '3',\n  l: '4',\n  m: '5', n: '5',\n  r: '6',\n};\n\nfunction phoneticKey(input: string): string {\n  const norm = normalize(input);\n  if (!norm) return '';\n  const first = norm[0];\n  let key = first.toUpperCase();\n  let lastCode = SOUNDEX_CODE[first] ?? '';\n  for (let i = 1; i < norm.length && key.length < 4; i++) {\n    const code = SOUNDEX_CODE[norm[i]] ?? '';\n    if (!code || code === lastCode) {\n      // skip vowels/h/w/y and avoid repeating the same class\n      if (!code) lastCode = '';\n      continue;\n    }\n    key += code;\n    lastCode = code;\n  }\n  return key.padEnd(4, '0');\n}\n\nfunction levenshtein(a: string, b: string): number {\n  if (a === b) return 0;\n  if (!a.length) return b.length;\n  if (!b.length) return a.length;\n  // rolling 1D array\n  let prev = new Array(b.length + 1);\n  for (let j = 0; j <= b.length; j++) prev[j] = j;\n  const curr = new Array(b.length + 1);\n  for (let i = 1; i <= a.length; i++) {\n    curr[0] = i;\n    for (let j = 1; j <= b.length; j++) {\n      const cost = a[i - 1] === b[j - 1] ? 0 : 1;\n      curr[j] = Math.min(\n        curr[j - 1] + 1,        // insertion\n        prev[j] + 1,            // deletion\n        prev[j - 1] + cost,     // substitution\n      );\n    }\n    prev = curr.slice();\n  }\n  return prev[b.length];\n}\n\nexport interface NameIndexEntry<T> {\n  item: T;\n  normalized: string;\n  phonetic: string;\n  tokens: string[]; // per-word normalized tokens (e.g. [\"junaid\",\"khan\"])\n}\n\nexport function buildNameIndex<T>(items: T[], getName: (item: T) => string): NameIndexEntry<T>[] {\n  return items.map((item) => {\n    const name = getName(item) ?? '';\n    const normalized = normalize(name);\n    const tokens = name\n      .split(/\\s+/)\n      .map(normalize)\n      .filter(Boolean);\n    return {\n      item,\n      normalized,\n      phonetic: phoneticKey(name),\n      tokens,\n    };\n  });\n}\n\nexport interface ScoredMatch<T> {\n  item: T;\n  score: number;\n}\n\n// Returns the top-N items ranked by similarity to the query.\n// Lower score = better. Returns empty list if query is empty.\nexport function rankByName<T>(\n  index: NameIndexEntry<T>[],\n  query: string,\n  limit = 5,\n): ScoredMatch<T>[] {\n  const q = normalize(query);\n  if (!q) return [];\n  const qPhonetic = phoneticKey(query);\n  const qTokens = query.split(/\\s+/).map(normalize).filter(Boolean);\n\n  const scored: ScoredMatch<T>[] = [];\n  for (const entry of index) {\n    if (!entry.normalized) continue;\n\n    // exact / prefix match on the whole name → strongest\n    let best = Infinity;\n    if (entry.normalized === q) best = 0;\n    else if (entry.normalized.startsWith(q)) best = 1;\n    else if (entry.normalized.includes(q)) best = 2;\n\n    // per-token prefix match (handles multi-word names: \"khan\" → \"junaid khan\")\n    for (const tok of entry.tokens) {\n      if (tok === q) best = Math.min(best, 0);\n      else if (tok.startsWith(q)) best = Math.min(best, 1);\n    }\n\n    // phonetic key match → strong signal for sound-alikes / typos\n    if (qPhonetic && entry.phonetic === qPhonetic) {\n      best = Math.min(best, 3);\n    }\n    for (const tok of entry.tokens) {\n      if (phoneticKey(tok) === qPhonetic) {\n        best = Math.min(best, 3);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // multi-token query: average best-per-token distance\n    if (qTokens.length > 1) {\n      let sum = 0;\n      for (const qt of qTokens) {\n        let local = Infinity;\n        for (const et of entry.tokens) {\n          local = Math.min(local, levenshtein(qt, et));\n        }\n        if (local === Infinity) local = qt.length;\n        sum += local;\n      }\n      best = Math.min(best, 4 + sum);\n    }\n\n    // Levenshtein fallback (scaled so it competes fairly with above)\n    const dist = levenshtein(q, entry.normalized);\n    const lenMax = Math.max(q.length, entry.normalized.length);\n    // Only consider edits worth ~30% of the longer string; further is noise.\n    if (dist <= Math.max(2, Math.ceil(lenMax * 0.3))) {\n      best = Math.min(best, 5 + dist);\n    }\n\n    if (best !== Infinity) {\n      scored.push({ item: entry.item, score: best });\n    }\n  }\n\n  scored.sort((a, b) => a.score - b.score);\n  return scored.slice(0, limit);\n}\n"
}

(res.data.results ?? []).slice(0, 5) : []);\n    }, 180);\n    return () => {\n      cancelled = true;\n      window.clearTimeout(timer);\n    };\n  }, [value]);",
  "new_string": "// In-memory + localStorage cache of the full staff directory, indexed for\n// the client-side fuzzy matcher. Fetched once per session (or whenever the\n// cache is older than STAFF_CACHE_TTL_MS). Keeps host autocomplete instant\n// and offline-friendly — no per-keystroke network call.\nconst STAFF_CACHE_KEY = 'guard_staff_directory_v1';\nconst STAFF_CACHE_TTL_MS = 6 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 6h\nlet staffIndexCache: { index: NameIndexEntry<StaffMember>[]; loadedAt: number } | null = null;\nlet staffIndexPromise: Promise<NameIndexEntry<StaffMember>[]> | null = null;\n\nfunction readStaffCacheFromStorage(): { staff: StaffMember[]; loadedAt: number } | null {\n  try {\n    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STAFF_CACHE_KEY);\n    if (!raw) return null;\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as { staff: StaffMember[]; loadedAt: number };\n    if (!Array.isArray(parsed.staff) || typeof parsed.loadedAt !== 'number') return null;\n    return parsed;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function loadStaffIndex(): Promise<NameIndexEntry<StaffMember>[]> {\n  if (staffIndexCache && Date.now() - staffIndexCache.loadedAt < STAFF_CACHE_TTL_MS) {\n    return staffIndexCache.index;\n  }\n  if (staffIndexPromise) return staffIndexPromise;\n\n  staffIndexPromise = (async () => {\n    // Serve stale cache from localStorage immediately so the first\n    // keystroke after a refresh is still instant; refresh in background.\n    const stored = readStaffCacheFromStorage();\n    if (stored && !staffIndexCache) {\n      staffIndexCache = { index: buildNameIndex(stored.staff, (s) => s.name), loadedAt: stored.loadedAt };\n    }\n\n    const res = await searchStaff('');\n    if (res.ok) {\n      const staff = res.data.results ?? [];\n      staffIndexCache = { index: buildNameIndex(staff, (s) => s.name), loadedAt: Date.now() };\n      try {\n        localStorage.setItem(STAFF_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify({ staff, loadedAt: staffIndexCache.loadedAt }));\n      } catch {\n        // storage quota or private mode — ignore\n      }\n    }\n    staffIndexPromise = null;\n    return staffIndexCache?.index ?? [];\n  })();\n\n  return staffIndexPromise;\n}\n\nfunction HostSuggestInput({\n  t,\n  value,\n  onChange,\n  required,\n}: {\n  t: (key: string) => string;\n  value: string;\n  onChange: (value: string) => void;\n  required?: boolean;\n}) {\n  const [index, setIndex] = useState<NameIndexEntry<StaffMember>[]>(() => staffIndexCache?.index ?? []);\n  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(() => !staffIndexCache);\n  const [focused, setFocused] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let cancelled = false;\n    void loadStaffIndex().then((next) => {\n      if (cancelled) return;\n      setIndex(next);\n      setLoading(false);\n    });\n    return () => {\n      cancelled = true;\n    };\n  }, []);\n\n  const suggestions = useMemo(() => {\n    const query = value.trim();\n    if (query.length < 2 || index.length === 0) return [];\n    return rankByName(index, query, 5).map((m) => m.item);\n  }, [index, value]);"
}

> AGENT

Done. Summary:

**New file** — `visitor-web/src/features/guard/nameMatch.ts`: pure utility with three layers — Devanagari→Latin transliteration table (~40 entries), Soundex-style phonetic key (first letter + 3 consonant-class digits), and Levenshtein distance. `buildNameIndex()` precomputes per-staff keys; `rankByName()` scores a query against the index.

**Modified** — `visitor-web/src/features/guard/GuardApp.tsx`:
- Added module-level cached staff directory loader (`loadStaffIndex`). Fetches `/api/staff/search?q=&limit=500` once per session, also persists to `localStorage` with a 6h TTL so a refresh is instant. Stale cache is served immediately while the refresh runs in the background.
- `HostSuggestInput` no longer does a network call per keystroke. It runs `rankByName(index, value, 5)` via `useMemo` — synchronous, microsecond range.
- Removed the 180ms debounce (no point with local matching).

**Trace on your examples:**
- "Junaid" → phonetic `J530`
- "Juneyd" → phonetic `J530` ✓
- "जुनैद" → transliterate → "junaid" → `J530` ✓
- "जुनेद" → "juned" → `J530` ✓
- "Khan" against entry "Junaid Khan" → token match on "khan" ✓
- "Sharma" vs "Sarma" → both `S650` ✓

**Cost / size:** zero deps, ~2.9 KB gzipped added to the bundle, zero per-query cost, works offline once cached.

**Tradeoff to keep in mind:** Soundex over-collapses some classes (c/g/j/k all → 2, b/f/p/v all → 1). If you start seeing wrong people ranked highly for short queries, switching to Double Metaphone (still hand-roll-able, ~150 lines) gives sharper separation without changing the architecture. Build passes; recommend `bun run dev` and trying it with a real staff list in the browser to confirm the rankings feel right per `visitor-web/CLAUDE.md`'s "verify before claiming done" guidance.

are there no changes on ops-engine?

> AGENT

For this fuzzy-match feature, no — it's intentionally pure-client. The backend already exposes the full directory via the existing `/api/staff/search?q=&limit=500` endpoint (`office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/visitor/routes.ts:3094` — default limit is 200, accepts up to 500). I'm reusing that exactly as-is, fetching once and matching locally. Nothing on the engine side needs to know about transliteration or phonetic keys.

The only ops-engine work I'd consider later is if you want to fall back to richer Indic matching after the client misses — that would be a Postgres `pg_trgm` query on staff names. But you said zero/low cost and the current client approach delivers that, so I'd hold off until we see real-world misses.

Heads up though: the **Slack + email host notification** feature from a few messages back never got built. We aligned on the plan (manual-entry route, respect the `notifyStakeholderRoles` / `notifyEmails` settings, Slack + email only, skip Telegram), I asked "OK to proceed?", and you pivoted to this matching question instead. Want me to circle back to that now?

```
Perfect! Now I have all the information needed. Let me compile my security audit report.

## SECURITY AUDIT REPORT: Visitor Module Endpoints

**CRITICAL VULNERABILITY FOUND:**

### 1. **Admin Secret Bypass (Lines 219-223, admin-auth.ts)**
If `VISITOR_ADMIN_SECRET` env var is **empty or unset** (defaults to `""`), the fallback at line 223 evaluates:
```typescript
const secretAccepted = configuredSecret ? providedSecret === configuredSecret : Boolean(providedSecret);
```
When `configuredSecret=""`, this becomes `Boolean(providedSecret)`. **Any non-empty header value for `x-admin-secret` grants full super-admin access**, even if it's just a dummy string like `"x"`. This turns the secret into a **present-but-bypassable** gate rather than a real auth barrier.

**Impact**: All `/api/admin/*` endpoints become unauthenticated if the secret is not explicitly configured.

---

### 2. **ENDPOINTS AUDITED:**

#### **`GET /api/staff/search` (line 3094)**
- **Auth gate**: `guardAuth(req)` (line 3095)
- **Failure path**: Returns 401 + short-circuits (line 3096) ✓
- **Implementation**: Token-based (line 230-233). Header: `x-guard-token` or query param `token`
- **guardAuth validation**: `validateGuardSession()` (guard-auth.ts:100-121)
  - Looks up token hash in DB, checks expiry, auto-extends session TTL
  - Session token = UUID, hashed with SHA256 before storage
  - Token lifetime: **24 hours**, rolling (touched on each access)
  - Revocation: `logoutGuard()` deletes token hash from DB ✓
- **Data returned**: Full staff directory (name, phone, email, employee code, role, department, attendance status, photo URL) — **up to 500 records**
- **Rate limiting**: None visible

#### **`GET /api/staff/suggest` (line 3125)**
- **Auth gate**: `guardAuth(req)` ✓
- **Failure path**: Returns 401 + short-circuits ✓
- **Data returned**: Cached staff subset (limit 25 by default) — same fields as `/staff/search`

#### **`POST /api/visitor/manual-entry` (line 3306)**
- **Auth gate**: `guardAuth(req)` ✓
- **Failure path**: Returns 401 + short-circuits ✓
- **Purpose**: Allows guards to log visitors directly (auto-approved, bypasses QR scan)

#### **`GET /api/admin/visitor/rules` (line 2661)**
- **Auth gate**: `requireAnyVisitorPermission(req, res, ['visitor.rules.manage', 'automation.rules.manage'])` (line 2662)
- **Failure path**: Returns 403 + short-circuits (routes.ts:274-276) ✓
- **Permission check**: `checkVisitorPermission()` (policies.ts:510-548)
  - Calls `resolveAdminSession(req)` which tries Bearer token first, then falls back to `x-admin-secret` header
  - **BUG**: If `VISITOR_ADMIN_SECRET` is unset/empty, any `x-admin-secret` header value becomes a valid super-admin token
  - Returns role-based permissions from DB or empty array for super-admin
- **Data returned**: Notification rules (conditions, actions, priority, enablement state)

#### **`POST /api/admin/visitor/rules` (line 2743)**
- **Auth gate**: `requireAnyVisitorPermission(req, res, ['visitor.rules.manage', 'automation.rules.manage'])` ✓
- **Failure path**: 403 + short-circuits ✓
- **Writeability**: Allows create/update of notification rules

#### **Other `/api/admin/visitor/*` Endpoints**
- `/api/admin/visitor/templates` (GET/POST/DELETE, lines 2671–2740): Protected by `requireVisitorPermission(...'visitor.templates.manage'|'view')` ✓
- `/api/admin/visitor/stakeholders` (GET/POST, lines 2814–2862): Protected by `requireVisitorPermission(...'visitor.stakeholders.view'|'manage')` ✓
- `/api/admin/visitor/broadcasts` (GET/POST, line 2877–2928): Protected by `requireVisitorPermission(...'visitor.broadcasts.view'|'manage')` ✓
- `/api/admin/visitor/sos` (line 2928+): Protected ✓

---

### 3. **UNPROTECTED ENDPOINTS (No Auth)**
- **`POST /api/visitor/otp/request`** (line 361): **NO AUTH** — Anyone can request OTP for any phone
- **`POST /api/visitor/otp/verify`** (line 382): **NO AUTH** — Anyone can verify OTP (open to brute-force)
- **`POST /api/visitor/register`** (line 406): **NO AUTH** — Walk-in registration, intentional

---

### 4. **CORS Configuration**
**File**: `server.ts` lines 74–90
```typescript
const VISITOR_HTTP_CORS_HEADERS = {
  'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',  // ← UNRESTRICTED
  'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET,POST,PUT,PATCH,DELETE,OPTIONS',
  'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': [...'Authorization', ...'x-admin-secret'...],
};
```
**Issue**: Origin is `*` — any domain can make authenticated requests on behalf of a user. Combined with the secret bypass above, an attacker site could exfiltrate staff data by forging `x-admin-secret: anything`.

---

### 5. **STAFF DATA SENSITIVITY**
No visible comments marking staff directory as "low-sensitivity." Returning **500 records** with name, phone, email, employee code, role, department, attendance, and photo URL constitutes **PII exposure**. A guard PWA user can enumerate the entire organization by calling `/api/staff/search?limit=500&q=`.

---

## PRIORITY FLAGS

**CRITICAL (Fix Immediately):**
1. **Admin secret fallback bypass** (admin-auth.ts:219-223) — if env is unset, any string bypasses auth
2. **CORS `Origin: *`** (server.ts:75) — allows cross-origin auth bypass
3. **Unprotected OTP endpoints** (lines 361, 382) — open to brute-force; no rate-limiting visible

**HIGH (Design Issue):**
4. **Staff directory limit=500** — guards can bulk-export full org via `/api/staff/search?limit=500&q=`. Consider whether this is intentional.
5. **Admin permissions without DB session** — `resolveAdminSession()` grants super-admin based only on a header when DB is unavailable; sessions are not logged.

**MODERATE:**
6. **Guard token: 24-hour TTL** — no MFA; single compromised PIN grants day-long access

---

**Files to review/fix:**
- `/src/mastra/modules/visitor/admin-auth.ts` lines 219–225
- `/src/mastra/server.ts` lines 74–90
- `/src/mastra/modules/visitor/visitor-otp.ts` (rate-limiting implementation)
```

Short answer for the endpoints we've touched: **yes, all gate on auth, but there's one real bug worth fixing in the admin path.**

**Verified clean:**
- `GET /api/staff/search` (`routes.ts:3094`) — `guardAuth(req)` → 401 short-circuit. Token is DB-backed, SHA256-hashed, 24h rolling TTL, revoked on logout.
- `GET /api/staff/suggest` (`routes.ts:3125`) — same.
- `POST /api/visitor/manual-entry` (`routes.ts:3306`) — same.
- `GET/POST /api/admin/visitor/rules` (`routes.ts:2661`, `2743`) — gated on `requireAnyVisitorPermission([visitor.rules.manage, automation.rules.manage])` → 403 on failure.

**Real bug found — admin secret bypass** (`office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/visitor/admin-auth.ts:219-223`):

```ts
const configuredSecret = process.env.VISITOR_ADMIN_SECRET ?? "";
const providedSecret = getHeader(req, "x-admin-secret");
const secretAccepted = configuredSecret
  ? providedSecret === configuredSecret
  : Boolean(providedSecret);
```

If `VISITOR_ADMIN_SECRET` is unset or empty, the ternary falls through to `Boolean(providedSecret)` — meaning **any non-empty `x-admin-secret` header value** (literally `x`) is accepted as super-admin. Combined with `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *` in `server.ts:75`, a malicious site could mint admin sessions on behalf of a victim browser if dev/prod env happens to leave the secret unset. The fix is one line: when no secret is configured, **deny** instead of accepting any header.

**Other things the audit flagged, less urgent for your work:**
- Staff directory is enumerable: a guard token can pull all ~500 staff records with name/phone/email/code. That's *by design* for autocomplete (and now the local fuzzy match also relies on it), but worth knowing if a guard PIN gets phished.
- Guard token has no MFA and a 24h rolling TTL.
- Visitor-side OTP endpoints (`/api/visitor/otp/request`, `/api/visitor/otp/verify`) are public — they should be, but I didn't verify rate-limiting there. Worth a separate audit if you haven't already.

Want me to patch the admin-secret bypass? It's a one-line fix and arguably belongs in a focused security commit on its own.

Build passed. Patched `office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/visitor/admin-auth.ts`:

- When `VISITOR_ADMIN_SECRET` is unset/empty, the header path now hard-denies — no more "any non-empty header = super-admin" fallback.

The 403 contract to callers stays the same — `resolveAdminSession` still returns `null`, which `requireVisitorPermission` / `requireAnyVisitorPermission` translates into the existing 403 response. No ripple changes needed.

// Vision-camera / ML Kit format names. Order doesn't affect decoding —

// dense GS1-128 SSCC labels, EAN/UPC products, QR, DataMatrix all work.

  'code-128',
  'code-39',
  'code-93',
  'codabar',
  'ean-13',
  'ean-8',
  'itf',
  'upc-a',
  'upc-e',
  'qr',
  'data-matrix',
  'pdf-417',
  'aztec',
] as const;

// ML Kit v4+ has strong internal confidence scoring. We no longer require
// a 2-read confirmation gate — that was the main cause of "I have to wave
// the barcode back and forth" sluggishness. A single valid decode is
// accepted immediately.

export function ScannerScreen({ navigation }: Props) {
  const { palette } = useTheme();
  const t = useT();
  const { hasPermission, requestPermission } = useCameraPermission();
  const device = useCameraDevice('back');
  const [torch, setTorch] = useState(false);
  const [manualOpen, setManualOpen] = useState(false);
  const [manualValue, setManualValue] = useState('');

  // acceptedRef — true while a code is being routed; blocks further reads.
  const acceptedRef = useRef(false);

  // Reset on every screen focus so returning from the order session flow
  // allows scanning the next item without remounting the screen.
  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      acceptedRef.current = false;
    }, []),
  );

  const flashOpacity = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const pulse = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!hasPermission) requestPermission();
  }, [hasPermission, requestPermission]);

  // Pulse the reticle border to communicate omnidirectional detection.
  useEffect(() => {
    const anim = Animated.loop(
      Animated.sequence([
        Animated.timing(pulse, {
          toValue: 1,
          duration: 900,
          easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.ease),
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }),
        Animated.timing(pulse, {

1	import Fuse from 'fuse.js';
2	import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
3	import { KnownProduct, listKnownProducts } from '../../../db/catalog';
4	
5	const FUSE_OPTIONS: ConstructorParameters<typeof Fuse<KnownProduct>>[1] = {
6	  keys: ['name'],
7	  threshold: 0.4, // 0 = exact, 1 = anything. 0.4 is a typo-tolerant sweet spot.
8	  ignoreLocation: true,
9	  minMatchCharLength: 2,
10	  includeScore: true,
11	};
12	
13	/**
14	 * Returns up to `limit` catalog products that fuzzy-match `query`.
15	 * Catalog is loaded once on first use and re-loaded whenever the
16	 * returned `reload()` is called (e.g. after a successful registration).
17	 *
18	 * Matching tolerates common typos ("watrmilan" → "Watermelon") but
19	 * cannot translate languages — that's Layer 3's job. If the user's
20	 * input is Hindi and no Hindi entry exists in the catalog yet, no
21	 * suggestions will fire.
22	 */
23	export function useCatalogSuggest(query: string, limit = 3) {
24	  const [catalog, setCatalog] = useState<KnownProduct[]>([]);
25	  const fuseRef = useRef<Fuse<KnownProduct> | null>(null);
26	
27	  const reload = useMemo(
28	    () => async () => {
29	      const rows = await listKnownProducts();
30	      setCatalog(rows);
31	    },
32	    [],
33	  );
34	
35	  useEffect(() => {
36	    reload().catch(() => {});
37	  }, [reload]);
38	
39	  useEffect(() => {
40	    fuseRef.current = new Fuse(catalog, FUSE_OPTIONS);
41	  }, [catalog]);
42	
43	  const suggestions = useMemo<KnownProduct[]>(() => {
44	    const q = query.trim();
45	    if (q.length < 2 || !fuseRef.current) return [];
46	    return fuseRef.current.search(q, { limit }).map((r) => r.item);
47	  }, [query, limit]);
48	
49	  return { suggestions, reload };
50	}
51

1	import { getDb, now } from './database';
2	
3	export type KnownProduct = {
4	  barcode: string;
5	  name: string;
6	  category: string | null;
7	  unit: string | null;
8	};
9	
10	/**
11	 * Union of every product the device knows about: locally-registered
12	 * products + every row mirrored from the remote stock cache. Used by
13	 * the fuzzy-suggest hook so the user can be offered an existing name
14	 * before they type it out from scratch (or misspell it).
15	 *
16	 * Dedups on barcode, preferring the locally-registered product entry
17	 * when both sources have it (it's the authoritative name on this device).
18	 */
19	export async function listKnownProducts(): Promise<KnownProduct[]> {
20	  const db = await getDb();
21	  const rows = await db.getAllAsync<KnownProduct>(`
22	    SELECT barcode, name, category, unit FROM products
23	    UNION
24	    SELECT barcode, name, category, unit FROM stock_levels
25	    WHERE barcode NOT IN (SELECT barcode FROM products)
26	    ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE
27	  `);
28	  return rows;
29	}
30	
31	// ─── Canonical Product Catalog ───────────────────────────────────
32	
33	export type CanonicalProduct = {
34	  product_id: string;
35	  canonical_name: string;
36	  category: string | null;
37	  hsn_code: string | null;
38	  unit: string;
39	  pack_size: number;
40	  updated_at: number;
41	};
42	
43	export async function listCanonicalProducts(): Promise<CanonicalProduct[]> {
44	  const db = await getDb();
45	  return db.getAllAsync<CanonicalProduct>(
46	    'SELECT * FROM canonical_products ORDER BY canonical_name',
47	  );
48	}
49	
50	export async function findCanonicalProductById(
51	  productId: string,
52	): Promise<CanonicalProduct | null> {
53	  const db = await getDb();
54	  return db.getFirstAsync<CanonicalProduct>(
55	    'SELECT * FROM canonical_products WHERE product_id = ?',
56	    [productId],
57	  );
58	}
59	
60	export async function findCanonicalProductByBarcode(
61	  barcode: string,
62	): Promise<CanonicalProduct | null> {
63	  const db = await getDb();
64	  return db.getFirstAsync<CanonicalProduct>(
65	    `SELECT cp.* FROM canonical_products cp
66	     JOIN product_barcodes pb ON pb.product_id = cp.product_id
67	     WHERE pb.barcode = ?`,
68	    [barcode],
69	  );
70	}
71	
72	export async function upsertCanonicalProduct(product: CanonicalProduct): Promise<void> {
73	  const db = await getDb();
74	  await db.runAsync(
75	    `INSERT INTO canonical_products (product_id, canonical_name, category, hsn_code, unit, pack_size, updated_at)
76	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
77	     ON CONFLICT(product_id) DO UPDATE SET
78	       canonical_name = excluded.canonical_name,
79	       category = excluded.category,
80	       hsn_code = excluded.hsn_code,
81	       unit = excluded.unit,
82	       pack_size = excluded.pack_size,
83	       updated_at = excluded.updated_at`,
84	    [
85	      product.product_id,
86	      product.canonical_name,
87	      product.category,
88	      product.hsn_code,
89	      product.unit,
90	      product.pack_size,
91	      product.updated_at,
92	    ],
93	  );
94	}
95	
96	/**
97	 * Replace the entire canonical product catalog with fresh data
98	 * from the backend. Used during full sync.
99	 *
100	 * IMPORTANT: do NOT `DELETE FROM canonical_products` first — there is a
101	 * cascading FK from `product_barcodes`, so a blanket delete blows away
102	 * every learned barcode → product mapping the guard has built up.
103	 * Instead we upsert each remote row, then delete the ones the remote
104	 * no longer knows about.
105	 */
106	export async function replaceCanonicalProducts(
107	  products: CanonicalProduct[],
108	): Promise<void> {
109	  const db = await getDb();
110	  for (const p of products) {
111	    await upsertCanonicalProduct(p);
112	  }
113	  if (products.length === 0) return; // empty payload — keep what we have
114	
115	  // Drop products the remote no longer has. Bind ids as literals (the
116	  // list is small and ids are alphanumeric); a parameterised IN with
117	  // dynamic length is painful in expo-sqlite.
118	  const ids = products.map((p) => `'${String(p.product_id).replace(/'/g, "''")}'`).join(',');
119	  await db.execAsync(`DELETE FROM canonical_products WHERE product_id NOT IN (${ids})`);
120	}
121	
122	// ─── Barcode Learning ────────────────────────────────────────────
123	
124	/**
125	 * Save a barcode → product mapping learned from a guard scan.
126	 * Next time this barcode is scanned, it auto-resolves.
127	 */
128	export async function learnBarcode(
129	  barcode: string,
130	  productId: string,
131	  learnedFrom: string = 'scan',
132	): Promise<void> {
133	  const db = await getDb();
134	  await db.runAsync(
135	    `INSERT INTO product_barcodes (barcode, product_id, learned_from, learned_at)
136	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
137	     ON CONFLICT(barcode) DO UPDATE SET
138	       product_id = excluded.product_id,
139	       learned_from = excluded.learned_from,
140	       learned_at = excluded.learned_at`,
141	    [barcode, productId, learnedFrom, now()],
142	  );
143	}
144	
145	// ─── Order Item Lookup by Product ID ─────────────────────────────
146	
147	export async function findOpenItemByProductId(
148	  productId: string,
149	): Promise<{
150	  id: string;
151	  order_id: string;
152	  product_id: string;
153	  product_name: string;
154	  expected_qty: number;
155	  received_qty: number;
156	} | null> {
157	  const db = await getDb();
158	  return db.getFirstAsync(
159	    `SELECT oi.* FROM order_items oi
160	     JOIN orders o ON o.id = oi.order_id
161	     WHERE oi.product_id = ? AND o.status = 'open'
162	     ORDER BY o.expected_at ASC
163	     LIMIT 1`,
164	    [productId],
165	  );
166	}
167
