```
1	import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
2	import { ScanLine } from 'lucide-react-native';
3	import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
4	import { ActivityIndicator, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
5	import {
6	  AppBar,
7	  Button,
8	  Chip,
9	  IconButton,
10	  radius,
11	  spacing,
12	  Text,
13	  TextField,
14	} from '../../../design';
15	import { enqueue } from '../../../db/outbox';
16	import { upsertProduct } from '../../../db/products';
17	import { useT } from '../../../i18n';
18	import { useTheme } from '../../../theme';
19	import { RootStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/types';
20	import { api } from '../../../sync/api';
21	import { flushOnce } from '../../../sync/syncService';
22	import { haptic } from '../../../utils/haptics';
23	import type { KnownProduct } from '../../../db/catalog';
24	import { useCatalogSuggest } from '../hooks/useCatalogSuggest';
25	
26	type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'RegisterProduct'>;
27	
28	const CATEGORIES = ['Grocery', 'Cleaning', 'Office', 'Cafeteria', 'Other'];
29	const UNITS = ['Piece', 'Kg', 'Litre', 'Pack', 'Box'];
30	
31	export function RegisterProductScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
32	  const t = useT();
33	  const { palette } = useTheme();
34	  const { barcode } = route.params;
35	  const [name, setName] = useState('');
36	  const [category, setCategory] = useState('Grocery');
37	  const [unit, setUnit] = useState('Piece');
38	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
39	  const canSave = name.trim().length > 0 && !saving;
40	  const { suggestions } = useCatalogSuggest(name);
41	
42	  const acceptSuggestion = (s: KnownProduct) => {
43	    haptic.tap();
44	    setName(s.name);
45	    if (s.category && CATEGORIES.includes(s.category)) setCategory(s.category);
46	    if (s.unit && UNITS.includes(s.unit)) setUnit(s.unit);
47	  };
48	
49	  // Layer 3: server-side LLM normalization. Debounced so we don't fire on
50	  // every keystroke — only after the user stops typing for ~350ms. Skipped
51	  // if there are local fuzzy matches (cheaper) or the input is too short.
52	  const [normalized, setNormalized] = useState<{ canonical: string } | null>(null);
53	  const [normalizing, setNormalizing] = useState(false);
54	  const normalizeReq = useRef(0);
55	  useEffect(() => {
56	    const trimmed = name.trim();
57	    if (trimmed.length < 3 || suggestions.length > 0) {
58	      setNormalized(null);
59	      setNormalizing(false);
60	      return;
61	    }
62	    const reqId = ++normalizeReq.current;
63	    const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
64	      // Only show the "Thinking…" pill if the fetch is still the latest.
65	      if (reqId !== normalizeReq.current) return;
66	      setNormalizing(true);
67	      try {
68	        const result = await api.fetch.normalizeName(trimmed);
69	        if (reqId !== normalizeReq.current) return;
70	        if (
71	          result.confidence >= 0.7 &&
72	          result.canonical.toLowerCase() !== trimmed.toLowerCase()
73	        ) {
74	          setNormalized({ canonical: result.canonical });
75	        } else {
76	          setNormalized(null);
77	        }
78	      } catch {
79	        if (reqId === normalizeReq.current) setNormalized(null);
80	      } finally {
81	        if (reqId === normalizeReq.current) setNormalizing(false);
82	      }
83	    }, 350);
84	    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
85	  }, [name, suggestions.length]);
86	
87	  const acceptNormalized = () => {
88	    if (!normalized) return;
89	    haptic.tap();
90	    setName(normalized.canonical);
91	    setNormalized(null);
92	  };
93	
94	  const onSave = async () => {
95	    if (!canSave) {
96	      haptic.warn();
97	      return;
98	    }
99	    setSaving(true);
100	    try {
101	      await upsertProduct({ barcode, name: name.trim(), category, unit });
102	      await enqueue('product_registration', { barcode, name: name.trim(), category, unit });
103	      flushOnce().catch(() => {});
104	      haptic.success();
105	      navigation.replace('Receiving', { barcode });
106	    } finally {
107	      setSaving(false);
108	    }
109	  };
110	
111	  return (
112	    <View style={[styles.safe, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>
113	      <AppBar
114	        title={t('newProduct')}
115	        subtitle={barcode}
116	        onBack={() => navigation.goBack()}
117	        trailing={<IconButton Icon={ScanLine} onPress={() => navigation.replace('Scanner')} />}
118	      />
119	      <KeyboardAvoidingView
120	        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
121	        style={{ flex: 1 }}
122	      >
123	        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scroll} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
124	          <Text variant="headlineMedium">{t('productName')}</Text>
125	          <Text variant="bodyLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
126	            Tell us once. We'll remember it next time you scan it.
127	          </Text>
128	
129	          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.xl }}>
130	            <TextField
131	              label={t('productName')}
132	              value={name}
133	              onChangeText={setName}
134	              placeholder={t('productExample')}
135	              autoFocus
136	              returnKeyType="done"
137	            />
138	            {suggestions.length > 0 && (
139	              <ScrollView
140	                horizontal
141	                showsHorizontalScrollIndicator={false}
142	                contentContainerStyle={styles.suggestRow}
143	                keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
144	              >
145	                <Text variant="labelMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={styles.suggestLabel}>
146	                  Did you mean
147	                </Text>
148	                {suggestions.map((s) => (
149	                  <Pressable
150	                    key={s.barcode}
151	                    onPress={() => acceptSuggestion(s)}
152	                    android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant }}
153	                    style={[styles.suggestChip, { backgroundColor: palette.primaryContainer }]}
154	                  >
155	                    <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onPrimaryContainer}>
156	                      {s.name}
157	                    </Text>
158	                  </Pressable>
159	                ))}
160	              </ScrollView>
161	            )}
162	            {suggestions.length === 0 && normalized && (
163	              <View style={styles.suggestRow}>
164	                <Text variant="labelMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={styles.suggestLabel}>
165	                  Did you mean
166	                </Text>
167	                <Pressable
168	                  onPress={acceptNormalized}
169	                  android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant }}
170	                  style={[styles.suggestChip, { backgroundColor: palette.primaryContainer }]}
171	                >
172	                  <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onPrimaryContainer}>
173	                    {normalized.canonical}
174	                  </Text>
175	                </Pressable>
176	              </View>
177	            )}
178	            {suggestions.length === 0 && !normalized && normalizing && (
179	              <View style={styles.suggestRow}>
180	                <ActivityIndicator color={palette.primary} size="small" />
181	                <Text variant="labelMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={styles.suggestLabel}>
182	                  Checking spelling…
183	                </Text>
184	              </View>
185	            )}
186	          </View>
187	
188	          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.xl }}>
189	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.sm }}>
190	              {t('category').toUpperCase()}
191	            </Text>
192	            <View style={styles.chipRow}>
193	              {CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
194	                <Chip key={c} label={c} selected={c === category} onPress={() => setCategory(c)} />
195	              ))}
196	            </View>
197	          </View>
198	
199	          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.xl }}>
200	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.sm }}>
201	              {t('unit').toUpperCase()}
202	            </Text>
203	            <View style={styles.chipRow}>
204	              {UNITS.map((u) => (
205	                <Chip key={u} label={u} selected={u === unit} onPress={() => setUnit(u)} />
206	              ))}
207	            </View>
208	          </View>
209	        </ScrollView>
210	
211	        <View style={[styles.footer, { backgroundColor: palette.surface, borderTopColor: palette.outlineVariant }]}>
212	          <Button
213	            label={t('saveAndContinue')}
214	            onPress={onSave}
215	            disabled={!canSave}
216	            loading={saving}
217	            size="lg"
218	            fullWidth
219	          />
220	          <Button
221	            label={t('cancel')}
222	            variant="text"
223	            onPress={() => navigation.popToTop()}
224	            style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm }}
225	          />
226	        </View>
227	      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
228	    </View>
229	  );
230	}
231	
232	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
233	  safe: { flex: 1 },
234	  scroll: { padding: spacing.xl, paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl },
235	  chipRow: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: spacing.sm },
236	  suggestRow: {
237	    flexDirection: 'row',
238	    alignItems: 'center',
239	    gap: spacing.sm,
240	    paddingTop: spacing.sm,
241	    paddingRight: spacing.sm,
242	  },
243	  suggestLabel: { marginRight: spacing.xs },
244	  suggestChip: {
245	    paddingHorizontal: spacing.md,
246	    paddingVertical: spacing.sm,
247	    borderRadius: radius.pill,
248	    overflow: 'hidden',
249	  },
250	  footer: {
251	    padding: spacing.xl,
252	    borderTopWidth: 1,
253	  },
254	});
255
```

```
1	import { getDb, now } from './database';
2	
3	export type Product = {
4	  barcode: string;
5	  name: string;
6	  category: string | null;
7	  unit: string | null;
8	  updated_at: number;
9	};
10	
11	export async function findProduct(barcode: string): Promise<Product | null> {
12	  const db = await getDb();
13	  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<Product>(
14	    'SELECT * FROM products WHERE barcode = ?',
15	    [barcode]
16	  );
17	  return row ?? null;
18	}
19	
20	export async function upsertProduct(p: Omit<Product, 'updated_at'>): Promise<Product> {
21	  const db = await getDb();
22	  const ts = now();
23	  await db.runAsync(
24	    `INSERT INTO products (barcode, name, category, unit, updated_at)
25	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
26	     ON CONFLICT(barcode) DO UPDATE SET
27	       name=excluded.name, category=excluded.category, unit=excluded.unit, updated_at=excluded.updated_at`,
28	    [p.barcode, p.name, p.category, p.unit, ts]
29	  );
30	  return { ...p, updated_at: ts };
31	}
32	
33	export async function listProducts(limit = 200): Promise<Product[]> {
34	  const db = await getDb();
35	  return db.getAllAsync<Product>(
36	    'SELECT * FROM products ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT ?',
37	    [limit]
38	  );
39	}
```

Before I start: the "old flow" you remember had name + category + unit type + unit value, but the current `RegisterProductScreen` only has **name + category + unit** (chip pick of Piece/Kg/Litre/Pack/Box). There's no separate "unit value" (numeric pack size) field today, even though the canonical catalog has `pack_size`.

Two interpretations:
1. By "unit type and unit value" you meant the existing single `unit` chip — nothing to add.
2. You want me to add a numeric **pack size** input next to the unit chip (so e.g. *Kg × 5* or *Litre × 1*).

Option 2 is the bigger change (SQLite schema migration on the `products` table, `upsertProduct` signature, outbox payload, backend acceptance for `pack_size` on `product_registration`, and pass-through to `Receiving`). Tell me which you want.

In the meantime I'll set up todos and do the two unambiguous parts: re-route scanner away from `CatalogPicker`, and upgrade the name search to fuzzy + phonetic + Hindi.

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/modules/inventory/hooks/nameMatch.ts",
  "content": "// Client-side fuzzy name matcher.\n// Cheap (no network), fast (microseconds), works offline, tolerates\n// Devanagari input + Latin sound-alikes / typos. Pure JS — RN-safe.\n//\n// Pipeline per candidate name:\n//   1. Lowercase + strip diacritics + transliterate any Devanagari\n//      to Latin (so \"घी\" becomes \"ghi\").\n//   2. Compute a Soundex-style phonetic key (so \"Ghee\" and \"Ghi\"\n//      collapse to the same key).\n//   3. Score: phonetic key match, substring / prefix match, then\n//      Levenshtein distance on the transliterated form as a fallback.\n//\n// Bundle cost: ~2KB minified, zero deps.\n\nconst DEVANAGARI_MAP: Record<string, string> = {\n  // independent vowels\n  'अ': 'a', 'आ': 'aa', 'इ': 'i', 'ई': 'ii', 'उ': 'u', 'ऊ': 'uu',\n  'ऋ': 'ri', 'ए': 'e', 'ऐ': 'ai', 'ओ': 'o', 'औ': 'au',\n  // consonants\n  'क': 'k', 'ख': 'kh', 'ग': 'g', 'घ': 'gh', 'ङ': 'ng',\n  'च': 'ch', 'छ': 'chh', 'ज': 'j', 'झ': 'jh', 'ञ': 'ny',\n  'ट': 't', 'ठ': 'th', 'ड': 'd', 'ढ': 'dh', 'ण': 'n',\n  'त': 't', 'थ': 'th', 'द': 'd', 'ध': 'dh', 'न': 'n',\n  'प': 'p', 'फ': 'ph', 'ब': 'b', 'भ': 'bh', 'म': 'm',\n  'य': 'y', 'र': 'r', 'ल': 'l', 'व': 'v',\n  'श': 'sh', 'ष': 'sh', 'स': 's', 'ह': 'h',\n  // matras\n  'ा': 'a', 'ि': 'i', 'ी': 'ii', 'ु': 'u', 'ू': 'uu',\n  'ृ': 'ri', 'े': 'e', 'ै': 'ai', 'ो': 'o', 'ौ': 'au',\n  // modifiers\n  'ं': 'n', 'ः': 'h', 'ँ': 'n',\n  '्': '', // virama suppresses the implicit 'a'\n  '़': '', // nukta — ignore for matching\n};\n\nfunction transliterate(input: string): string {\n  let out = '';\n  for (const ch of input) {\n    out += DEVANAGARI_MAP[ch] ?? ch;\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nfunction normalize(input: string): string {\n  return transliterate(input)\n    .normalize('NFD')\n    .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '')\n    .toLowerCase()\n    .replace(/[^a-z]+/g, '');\n}\n\nconst SOUNDEX_CODE: Record<string, string> = {\n  b: '1', f: '1', p: '1', v: '1',\n  c: '2', g: '2', j: '2', k: '2', q: '2', s: '2', x: '2', z: '2',\n  d: '3', t: '3',\n  l: '4',\n  m: '5', n: '5',\n  r: '6',\n};\n\nfunction phoneticKey(input: string): string {\n  const norm = normalize(input);\n  if (!norm) return '';\n  const first = norm[0];\n  let key = first.toUpperCase();\n  let lastCode = SOUNDEX_CODE[first] ?? '';\n  for (let i = 1; i < norm.length && key.length < 4; i++) {\n    const code = SOUNDEX_CODE[norm[i]] ?? '';\n    if (!code || code === lastCode) {\n      if (!code) lastCode = '';\n      continue;\n    }\n    key += code;\n    lastCode = code;\n  }\n  return key.padEnd(4, '0');\n}\n\nfunction levenshtein(a: string, b: string): number {\n  if (a === b) return 0;\n  if (!a.length) return b.length;\n  if (!b.length) return a.length;\n  let prev = new Array(b.length + 1);\n  for (let j = 0; j <= b.length; j++) prev[j] = j;\n  const curr = new Array(b.length + 1);\n  for (let i = 1; i <= a.length; i++) {\n    curr[0] = i;\n    for (let j = 1; j <= b.length; j++) {\n      const cost = a[i - 1] === b[j - 1] ? 0 : 1;\n      curr[j] = Math.min(\n        curr[j - 1] + 1,\n        prev[j] + 1,\n        prev[j - 1] + cost,\n      );\n    }\n    prev = curr.slice();\n  }\n  return prev[b.length];\n}\n\nexport interface NameIndexEntry<T> {\n  item: T;\n  normalized: string;\n  phonetic: string;\n  tokens: string[];\n}\n\nexport function buildNameIndex<T>(items: T[], getName: (item: T) => string): NameIndexEntry<T>[] {\n  return items.map((item) => {\n    const name = getName(item) ?? '';\n    const normalized = normalize(name);\n    const tokens = name\n      .split(/\\s+/)\n      .map(normalize)\n      .filter(Boolean);\n    return {\n      item,\n      normalized,\n      phonetic: phoneticKey(name),\n      tokens,\n    };\n  });\n}\n\nexport interface ScoredMatch<T> {\n  item: T;\n  score: number;\n}\n\nexport function rankByName<T>(\n  index: NameIndexEntry<T>[],\n  query: string,\n  limit = 5,\n): ScoredMatch<T>[] {\n  const q = normalize(query);\n  if (!q) return [];\n  const qPhonetic = phoneticKey(query);\n  const qTokens = query.split(/\\s+/).map(normalize).filter(Boolean);\n\n  const scored: ScoredMatch<T>[] = [];\n  for (const entry of index) {\n    if (!entry.normalized) continue;\n\n    let best = Infinity;\n    if (entry.normalized === q) best = 0;\n    else if (entry.normalized.startsWith(q)) best = 1;\n    else if (entry.normalized.includes(q)) best = 2;\n\n    for (const tok of entry.tokens) {\n      if (tok === q) best = Math.min(best, 0);\n      else if (tok.startsWith(q)) best = Math.min(best, 1);\n    }\n\n    if (qPhonetic && entry.phonetic === qPhonetic) {\n      best = Math.min(best, 3);\n    }\n    for (const tok of entry.tokens) {\n      if (phoneticKey(tok) === qPhonetic) {\n        best = Math.min(best, 3);\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (qTokens.length > 1) {\n      let sum = 0;\n      for (const qt of qTokens) {\n        let local = Infinity;\n        for (const et of entry.tokens) {\n          local = Math.min(local, levenshtein(qt, et));\n        }\n        if (local === Infinity) local = qt.length;\n        sum += local;\n      }\n      best = Math.min(best, 4 + sum);\n    }\n\n    const dist = levenshtein(q, entry.normalized);\n    const lenMax = Math.max(q.length, entry.normalized.length);\n    if (dist <= Math.max(2, Math.ceil(lenMax * 0.3))) {\n      best = Math.min(best, 5 + dist);\n    }\n\n    if (best !== Infinity) {\n      scored.push({ item: entry.item, score: best });\n    }\n  }\n\n  scored.sort((a, b) => a.score - b.score);\n  return scored.slice(0, limit);\n}\n"
}
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/modules/inventory/hooks/useCatalogSuggest.ts",
  "content": "import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';\nimport { KnownProduct, listKnownProducts } from '../../../db/catalog';\nimport { buildNameIndex, rankByName, type NameIndexEntry } from './nameMatch';\n\n/**\n * Returns up to `limit` catalog products that fuzzy-match `query`.\n * Catalog is loaded once on first use and re-loaded whenever the\n * returned `reload()` is called (e.g. after a successful registration).\n *\n * Matching layers (cheapest first, all client-side):\n *   - normalized substring / prefix match\n *   - Soundex-style phonetic key match (so \"ghee\" finds \"ghi\", and\n *     a Hindi input like \"घी\" — transliterated to \"ghi\" — also hits)\n *   - Levenshtein distance fallback for typos (\"watrmilan\" → \"Watermelon\")\n *\n * Zero network calls, no extra deps.\n */\nexport function useCatalogSuggest(query: string, limit = 3) {\n  const [catalog, setCatalog] = useState<KnownProduct[]>([]);\n\n  const reload = useMemo(\n    () => async () => {\n      const rows = await listKnownProducts();\n      setCatalog(rows);\n    },\n    [],\n  );\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    reload().catch(() => {});\n  }, [reload]);\n\n  const index = useMemo<NameIndexEntry<KnownProduct>[]>(\n    () => buildNameIndex(catalog, (p) => p.name ?? ''),\n    [catalog],\n  );\n\n  const suggestions = useMemo<KnownProduct[]>(() => {\n    const q = query.trim();\n    if (q.length < 2 || index.length === 0) return [];\n    return rankByName(index, q, limit).map((r) => r.item);\n  }, [index, query, limit]);\n\n  return { suggestions, reload };\n}\n"
}
```

> AGENT

Done with the two unambiguous parts.

**Changes:**

1. **`field-app/src/modules/inventory/screens/ScannerScreen.tsx:162`** — Post-scan miss now navigates to `RegisterProduct` instead of `CatalogPicker`. The other `CatalogPicker` reference (the explicit "No barcode" button at line 335) is intentional opt-in browsing, left alone.

2. **`field-app/src/modules/inventory/hooks/nameMatch.ts`** (new) — Same utility I built for the visitor-web host autocomplete: Devanagari→Latin transliteration table + Soundex-style phonetic key + Levenshtein. Pure JS, no DOM, runs in RN unchanged. Zero deps, ~2KB.

3. **`field-app/src/modules/inventory/hooks/useCatalogSuggest.ts`** — Same hook signature, but internals now build a name index from `listKnownProducts()` and call `rankByName()`. Drops the `fuse.js` import. `RegisterProductScreen` doesn't need any changes — it already consumes `{ suggestions, reload }`.

**Matcher behavior on your examples:**
- "ghee" → catalog "Ghee" → exact phonetic match (`G000`)
- "घी" → transliterate → "ghi" → same phonetic key as "Ghee" → match
- "ghi" or "gee" → phonetic match
- "watrmilan" → "Watermelon" via Levenshtein (dist 2, within 30% threshold)
- Multi-word "amul ghee" vs "Amul Pure Ghee" → token-level matching

**Notes:**
- `fuse.js` is now an unused dependency in `field-app/package.json`. Want me to remove it, or leave it for a separate cleanup pass?
- `npm run lint` (tsc --noEmit) passes. Per `field-app/CLAUDE.md`, the routing change is JS-only — Metro hot-reload will pick it up; no dev-client rebuild needed. Worth a real-phone smoke test of: scan a known barcode → Receiving; scan an unknown one → RegisterProduct; type "ghee"/"घी" → see suggestions.
- **Still need your call on "unit value":** add a numeric pack-size field to `RegisterProductScreen` (schema migration + outbox + backend) or no?

```
86	/**
87	 * Forward-only migrations keyed by PRAGMA user_version. Each migration
88	 * runs once; version is bumped after success. ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN is
89	 * idempotent in SQLite (errors if column exists), so we guard with the
90	 * version check rather than catching errors.
91	 */
92	async function runMigrations(db: SQLite.SQLiteDatabase): Promise<void> {
93	  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<{ user_version: number }>(
94	    'PRAGMA user_version',
95	  );
96	  let version = row?.user_version ?? 0;
97	
98	  if (version < 1) {
99	    await db.execAsync(`
100	      -- Per-scan expiry and audit columns on receipts
101	      ALTER TABLE receipts ADD COLUMN expiry_date TEXT;
102	      ALTER TABLE receipts ADD COLUMN performed_by TEXT;
103	      ALTER TABLE receipts ADD COLUMN performed_by_name TEXT;
104	
105	      -- Audit trail table
106	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS activity_log (
107	        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
108	        action TEXT NOT NULL,
109	        barcode TEXT,
110	        qty REAL,
111	        expiry_date TEXT,
112	        performed_by TEXT,
113	        performed_by_name TEXT,
114	        performed_at INTEGER NOT NULL
115	      );
116	
117	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_activity_action ON activity_log(action);
118	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_activity_barcode ON activity_log(barcode);
119	
120	      PRAGMA user_version = 1;
121	    `);
122	    version = 1;
123	  }
124	
125	  // Future migrations go here:
126	  // if (version < 3) { ... PRAGMA user_version = 3; }
127	
128	  if (version < 2) {
129	    await db.execAsync(`
130	      -- Track nearest expiry per stock item for quick alert queries
131	      ALTER TABLE stock_levels ADD COLUMN nearest_expiry TEXT;
132	
133	      -- Per-receipt expiry tracking. Each received item with an expiry date
134	      -- gets a row here. This lets us query "what's expiring in the next N days"
135	      -- and trace it back to the order/receipt it came from.
136	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS expiry_tracking (
137	        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
138	        barcode TEXT NOT NULL,
139	        product_name TEXT NOT NULL,
140	        expiry_date TEXT NOT NULL,
141	        qty REAL NOT NULL,
142	        receipt_id TEXT,
143	        received_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
144	        performed_by TEXT,
145	        performed_by_name TEXT
146	      );
147	
148	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_expiry_barcode ON expiry_tracking(barcode);
149	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_expiry_date ON expiry_tracking(expiry_date);
150	
151	      PRAGMA user_version = 2;
152	    `);
153	    version = 2;
154	  }
155	
156	  if (version < 3) {
157	    await db.execAsync(`
158	      -- Canonical product catalog (synced from backend).
159	      -- Guards pick from this list — no free-text product names.
160	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS canonical_products (
161	        product_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
162	        canonical_name TEXT NOT NULL,
163	        category TEXT,
164	        hsn_code TEXT,
165	        unit TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pcs',
166	        pack_size REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
167	        updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
168	      );
169	
170	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_canonical_name ON canonical_products(canonical_name);
171	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_canonical_hsn ON canonical_products(hsn_code);
172	
173	      -- Learned barcode → canonical product mappings.
174	      -- Populated when guard scans a barcode + picks from catalog.
175	      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS product_barcodes (
176	        barcode TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
177	        product_id TEXT NOT NULL,
178	        learned_from TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'scan',
179	        learned_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
180	        FOREIGN KEY (product_id) REFERENCES canonical_products(product_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
181	      );
182	
183	      -- Link order items to canonical products for name-based matching
184	      ALTER TABLE order_items ADD COLUMN product_id TEXT;
185	
186	      CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_items_product ON order_items(product_id);
187	
188	      PRAGMA user_version = 3;
189	    `);
190	    version = 3;
191	  }
192	}
193	
194	export function now(): number {
195	  return Date.now();
196	}
```

```
81	export async function upsertProduct(input: {
82	  siteId: string;
83	  barcode: string;
84	  name: string;
85	  category?: string | null;
86	  unit?: string | null;
87	  registeredBy?: string | null;
88	}): Promise<void> {
89	  const db = await getDb();
90	  await db.execute({
91	    sql: `
92	      INSERT INTO inventory_products (barcode, site_id, name, category, unit, registered_by)
93	      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
94	      ON CONFLICT (site_id, barcode) DO UPDATE SET
95	        name = EXCLUDED.name,
96	        category = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.category, inventory_products.category),
97	        unit = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.unit, inventory_products.unit),
98	        updated_at = now()
99	    `,
100	    args: [
101	      input.barcode,
102	      input.siteId,
103	      input.name,
104	      input.category ?? null,
105	      input.unit ?? null,
106	      input.registeredBy ?? null,
107	    ],
108	  });
109	}
110	
111	export async function findProduct(
112	  siteId: string,
113	  barcode: string,
114	): Promise<InventoryProduct | null> {
115	  const db = await getDb();
116	  const result = await db.execute({
117	    sql: `SELECT * FROM inventory_products WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? LIMIT 1`,
118	    args: [siteId, barcode],
119	  });
120	  return (result.rows[0] as InventoryProduct | undefined) ?? null;
121	}
122	
123	// ─── Orders ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
124	
125	export async function listOpenOrders(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryOrder[]> {
126	  const db = await getDb();
127	  const result = await db.execute({
128	    sql: `SELECT * FROM inventory_orders WHERE site_id = ? AND status = 'open' ORDER BY expected_at ASC NULLS LAST`,
129	    args: [siteId],
130	  });
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/db/products.ts",
  "content": "import { getDb, now } from './database';\n\nexport type Product = {\n  barcode: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string | null;\n  unit: string | null;\n  pack_size: number | null;\n  updated_at: number;\n};\n\nexport async function findProduct(barcode: string): Promise<Product | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<Product>(\n    'SELECT * FROM products WHERE barcode = ?',\n    [barcode]\n  );\n  return row ?? null;\n}\n\nexport async function upsertProduct(p: Omit<Product, 'updated_at'>): Promise<Product> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const ts = now();\n  await db.runAsync(\n    `INSERT INTO products (barcode, name, category, unit, pack_size, updated_at)\n     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n     ON CONFLICT(barcode) DO UPDATE SET\n       name=excluded.name,\n       category=excluded.category,\n       unit=excluded.unit,\n       pack_size=excluded.pack_size,\n       updated_at=excluded.updated_at`,\n    [p.barcode, p.name, p.category, p.unit, p.pack_size, ts]\n  );\n  return { ...p, updated_at: ts };\n}\n\nexport async function listProducts(limit = 200): Promise<Product[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  return db.getAllAsync<Product>(\n    'SELECT * FROM products ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT ?',\n    [limit]\n  );\n}\n"
}
```

```
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { StyleSheet, TextInput, TextInputProps, View } from 'react-native';
import { useTheme } from '../theme';
import { Text } from './Text';
import { radius, spacing, type as typo } from './tokens';

type Props = TextInputProps & {
  label: string;
  helper?: string;
  error?: string;
};

export function TextField({ label, helper, error, onFocus, onBlur, style, ...rest }: Props) {
  const { palette } = useTheme();
  const [focused, setFocused] = useState(false);
  const borderColor = error
    ? palette.error
    : focused
    ? palette.primary
    : palette.outlineVariant;
  return (
    <View>
      <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.xs }}>
        {label}
      </Text>
      <TextInput
        {...rest}
        onFocus={(e) => {
          setFocused(true);
          onFocus?.(e);
        }}
        onBlur={(e) => {
          setFocused(false);
          onBlur?.(e);
        }}
        placeholderTextColor={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
        style={[
          styles.input,
          typo.bodyLarge,
          {
```

```json
  "new_string": "  const { barcode } = route.params;\n  const [name, setName] = useState('');\n  const [category, setCategory] = useState('Grocery');\n  const [unit, setUnit] = useState('Piece');\n  const [packSize, setPackSize] = useState('');\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n  const canSave = name.trim().length > 0 && !saving;\n  const { suggestions } = useCatalogSuggest(name);\n\n  const acceptSuggestion = (s: KnownProduct) => {\n    haptic.tap();\n    setName(s.name);\n    if (s.category && CATEGORIES.includes(s.category)) setCategory(s.category);\n    if (s.unit && UNITS.includes(s.unit)) setUnit(s.unit);\n    if (s.pack_size != null && s.pack_size > 0) setPackSize(String(s.pack_size));\n  };\n\n  // Allow only digits and a single decimal point in the pack-size input.\n  const onPackSizeChange = (raw: string) => {\n    const cleaned = raw.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '');\n    const parts = cleaned.split('.');\n    setPackSize(parts.length > 1 ? `${parts[0]}.${parts.slice(1).join('')}` : cleaned);\n  };\n\n  const parsedPackSize = (() => {\n    const n = parseFloat(packSize);\n    return Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n : null;\n  })();"
}
```

> AGENT

Both ends green. Summary of the unit-value addition:

**field-app**
- `db/database.ts`: schema bumped to v4, migration adds `pack_size REAL` to `products`.
- `db/products.ts`: `Product` gains `pack_size: number | null`; `upsertProduct` persists it.
- `db/catalog.ts`: `KnownProduct` + `listKnownProducts` UNION now include `pack_size` (NULL from `stock_levels`).
- `sync/api.ts`: `RemoteProduct.pack_size` field added.
- `screens/RegisterProductScreen.tsx`: new "Unit value" `TextField` with `decimal-pad` keyboard, digit/single-dot sanitiser, `parsedPackSize` (positive number or null). Passed to `upsertProduct`, the `product_registration` outbox payload, and `Receiving` navigation. `acceptSuggestion` now copies `pack_size` from the picked suggestion.
- `screens/ScannerScreen.tsx`: when the backend catalog hit returns a `pack_size`, it's persisted locally and forwarded to `Receiving`.
- `i18n/strings.ts`: new `unitValue` / `unitValueHint` keys (Hindi + English).

**ops-engine**
- `lib/postgres-schema.ts`: `inventory_products` gets `pack_size REAL` in the CREATE (fresh DBs) and an `ALTER TABLE … ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pack_size REAL` for existing DBs.
- `modules/inventory/db.ts`: `InventoryProduct` type and `upsertProduct({ packSize? })` updated; `COALESCE(EXCLUDED.pack_size, ...)` preserves a previously-set value if a later write omits it.
- `modules/inventory/routes.ts`: `POST /api/inventory/products` now reads `body.packSize` / `body.pack_size`, accepts only positive numbers. The catalog GET passes pack_size through automatically via `SELECT *`.

`npm run lint` (field-app) and `npm run build` (ops-engine) both pass. Per `field-app/CLAUDE.md`, the SQLite migration runs once on next app start — worth a sanity check on a phone that already has v3 data to confirm the column add lands without dropping rows.
